## Objective

Deliver the approved multi-session singleton MCP runtime and workspace-pinned all-tools environment specified in `docs/product-specs/2026-11-04-multi-session-mcp-runtime-requirements.md` and decided in `docs/decisions/2026-11-04-multi-session-mcp-runtime-deliberation.md` (`082-DL`, anchor stash `ECF33C79`), structured across three outcome-oriented covering features and three queued shipments:

* **Track B — Feature `211-F` / Shipment `212-S` (`Backlogit Multi-Session Singleton MCP Runtime, Stdio Session Shim, and Channel Operation Coordinator`)**:
  * Evolve `backlogit mcp` from a single-process exclusion guard (`204-F` / `205-S`) into an **independent per-workspace background daemon** (`backlogit mcp daemon`) paired with drop-in `stdio`-to-IPC session shims (`backlogit mcp`) and idempotent startup prewarm (`backlogit mcp prewarm`, `R1`–`R6`, `R11`–`R17`).
  * Gate all `backlogit` runtime-managed MCP mutations sequentially in deterministic FIFO order via an internal Go channel `OperationCoordinator` (`OpMutating`, `OpReadOnly`, `OpExclusiveBarrier`) with synchronous-frame turn/lock ownership (`R7`–`R9`, `R12`) and capability-gated `notifications/progress` emission (`R10`).
  * Replace exclusive lock probing in `InspectMCPDaemonStatus` and session connect handshakes with non-exclusive sidecar + process-identity + IPC readiness probing (`R3`, `D5`, `A045DBA4`).
* **Track A — Feature `212-F` / Shipment `213-S` (`Workspace Tool-Environment Inventory, Level 1 Version-Pinning Schema, and Threat-Model Contract`)**:
  * Define the cross-tool workspace toolchain inventory schema (`schemas/workspace-toolchain.schema.json`) and Go verification package (`internal/toolchain`) enforcing **Level 1 containment strength** (workspace-local installation, configuration, and version pinning across all workspace tools with zero silent global `PATH` or cross-workspace fallback, explicit `host-prerequisite` and `remote-declared` declarations, and honest `built-in-host`, `unsupported`, and `non-conformant` reporting, `R18`–`R23`, `D7`, `D9`).
* **Track C — Feature `213-F` / Shipment `214-S` (`Cross-Tool Workspace Pinning Verifier, Launcher/Companion MCP Alignment, and Multi-Workspace Negative Verification`)**:
  * Integrate Track A and Track B into `backlogit mcp status --toolchain [--strict]` and `internal/toolchain/launcher.go` (`VerifyLauncherScripts`, `AuditRepositoryToolchain`, `WriteToolchainManifest`, `RenderPinnedMCPConfig`), commit the repository's canonical tracked `.backlogit/toolchain.yaml` manifest, define the actionable operator-owned verified workspace toolchain setup/provisioning and custody alignment procedure across `.mcp.json`, `start.ps1`, `start.sh`, `bin/`, and `tools/mcp/` (`engram`, `graphtor-docs`/`kbdb`, `context7`, `tavily`, `github`, `bun`/`bunx`), and verify with end-to-end multi-workspace isolation and local-pinning negative test suites (`R19`–`R23`, `SC7`–`SC14`).

## Problem Frame

### Codebase and SDK Grounding

1. **Why `204-F` (`205-S`) and `mcp-go v0.27.0` `StdioServer` Cannot Host Multiple Concurrent Agents**:
   * `internal/cli/root.go:467` (`newMCPCommand`) currently calls `AcquireMCPDaemonLock(cmd.Context(), *cwd, replace)` (`internal/cli/mcp_daemon.go:265`) and then `serveMCPStdio(s)` (`internal/mcp/server.go:132`, `mcpserver.ServeStdio(s.mcp)`).
   * When a second agent (e.g., Stage running alongside Orchestrator, Ship running alongside an IDE session, or parallel review subagents) invokes `./bin/backlogit.exe mcp`, `AcquireMCPDaemonLock` fails immediately with `corerrors.ErrDaemonAlreadyRunning` unless `--replace` is passed—which terminates the first agent's active MCP server.
   * Direct source inspection of `github.com/mark3labs/mcp-go v0.27.0` (`server/stdio.go:53-77, 197-222`) confirms two SDK constraints in `StdioServer`:
     * `StdioServer.Listen` registers a package-global static session (`&stdioSessionInstance`, `SessionID() == "stdio"`). Calling `Listen` a second time on the same `*mcpserver.MCPServer` returns `ErrSessionExists`.
     * `StdioServer.processInputStream` invokes `s.server.HandleMessage(ctx, rawMessage)` synchronously in its line-reading loop (`stdio.go:139, 236`), and `MCPServer.HandleMessage` (`server/request_handler.go:45-56`) does not maintain a per-session in-flight request cancellation table for `notifications/cancelled`.
   * However, `mcpserver.MCPServer` itself (`server/session.go:40-185`) stores sessions in a `sync.Map` keyed by `session.SessionID()`, marks the session initialized inside `handleInitialize` (`server/server.go:530`), and routes `SendNotificationToClient(ctx, method, params)` to `ClientSessionFromContext(ctx).NotificationChannel()` (`chan<- mcplib.JSONRPCNotification`). Constructing a per-connection `ClientSession` implementation (`MultiSessionConn`) with a unique `sessionID`, an internal bidirectional `notifications chan mcplib.JSONRPCNotification` field (returned as send-only `NotificationChannel() chan<- mcplib.JSONRPCNotification` to satisfy `mcpserver.ClientSession` while `ServeSessionStream` drains `<-sess.notifications`), a mutex-serialized line writer, and a per-session `inFlight` request map (`requestId -> context.CancelFunc`) provides complete per-session request, notification, and cancellation isolation over a single `*mcpserver.MCPServer`.
2. **In-Memory State and Concurrency Surfaces in `internal/mcp/Server`**:
   * `Server` (`internal/mcp/server.go:27-62`) holds `RootPath`, `Workspace *core.Workspace` (with a 4-connection WAL-mode `*sql.DB` pool configured in `internal/db/connection.go:75-76` with `busy_timeout(30000)` and `_txlock=immediate`), `Events *events.EventWriter`, `Telemetry *events.TelemetryWriter`, `HookEvents *events.HookEventWriter`, `templateSvc *templates.Service`, `workspaceMu sync.Mutex`, and `manifest map[string]db.FileEntry` guarded by `manifestMu sync.RWMutex`.
   * Two subtle concurrency hazards exist in `internal/mcp` today if multiple sessions call handlers concurrently:
     * `handleListTemplates` (`internal/mcp/dynamic.go:31`) reads `s.templateSvc` without holding `s.workspaceMu`, racing with `ensureWorkspace` (`internal/mcp/server.go:167`) which calls `s.refreshTemplateService(ctx)` and mutates `s.templateSvc` under `s.workspaceMu`.
     * `handlePollHookEvents` and `handleAckHookEvents` (`internal/mcp/hook_tools.go:71-118`) read `s.HookEvents` and `s.backlogitDir()` without calling `s.ensureWorkspace(ctx)` or locking `s.workspaceMu`, racing with `ensureWorkspace` (`server.go:180`) which reassigns `s.HookEvents` and `s.Telemetry` when an initially uninitialized workspace is later initialized on disk.
   * Furthermore, mutating tool handlers in `internal/mcp/tools.go` perform multi-step file, manifest, event-log, and SQLite writes without an internal FIFO admission queue, while `backlogit_sync_index` (`handleSyncIndex`, `tools.go:920`) runs `core.MigrateDBOnlyLinksBeforeRehydrate` + `db.Rehydrate` across the entire SQLite database without draining concurrent in-process readers.
3. **Exclusive Lock-Probe Contention in `InspectMCPDaemonStatus` (`A045DBA4` / `R3` / `D5`)**:
   * `InspectMCPDaemonStatus` (`internal/cli/mcp_daemon.go:339-374`) currently probes liveness by calling `openMCPDaemonLockFile(runtimeRoot)` (`tryLockFile`), which takes a non-blocking **exclusive kernel lock** on `mcp-daemon.lock`. If `backlogit mcp status` or a connecting shim probes `mcp-daemon.lock` at the same instant a daemon is starting up and calling `AcquireMCPDaemonLock`, the status probe's transient exclusive lock causes `AcquireMCPDaemonLock` to fail spuriously with `ErrDaemonAlreadyRunning`.
4. **All-Tools Workspace Environment and Dirty Worktree Baseline (`R19`–`R23`, `D9`)**:
   * Inspection of `.mcp.json`, `start.ps1`, `start.sh`, `bin/`, `.copilot/graphtor-mcp-shim.cjs`, and `tools/mcp/` shows:
     * `.mcp.json` pins `backlogit` to `"./bin/backlogit.exe"`, but uses bare command names for `"engram"` (`"command": "engram"`), `"graphtor-docs"` (`"command": "bun"`, `"args": [".copilot\\graphtor-mcp-shim.cjs", "graphtor-docs", ...]`), `"context7"`, `"tavily"`, and `"github"` (`"command": "github-mcp-server.exe"`), even though `bin/engram.exe`, `bin/bun.exe`, `bin/bunx.exe`, `bin/github-mcp-server.exe`, and `bin/kbdb.exe` exist in `./bin/`.
     * Pre-existing uncommitted edits exist in `.autoharness/config.yaml`, `.gitignore`, `start.ps1`, `tools/`, and unrelated stash entry `D895887C` in `.backlogit/stash.jsonl`. Stage MUST NOT modify or commit those unrelated dirty files, and the plan's toolchain verifier (`internal/toolchain`) must inspect and report workspace tool compliance honestly without mutating dirty operator files or claiming control over upstream external repositories.
5. **Windows Go 1.24 Nested `os.Root.Name()` Relative Runtime Path Defect (`C2CFE06E` / `085-DL` / `U1b` `211.008-T`)**:
   * During Wave 1 convergence of `212-S` (`docs/memory/2026-11-06/212-S-wave1-convergence-blocked.md`, `docs/compound/runtime-errors/nested-os-root-name-is-not-an-absolute-path-2026-11-06.md`), the unfiltered Go 1.24.0 test gate (`go test -timeout=30m ./...`) failed 16 existing daemon-related tests in `./internal/cli` (`TestU3_StopMCPDaemon_NotRunning`, `TestU2_AcquireLockAndContention`, `TestU3_StopMCPDaemon_TerminatesVerifiedHolder`, `TestU4_CrossProcessMCPDaemonLockAndStop`, etc.).
   * At `internal/cli/mcp_daemon.go:177`, `storageHandle.OpenRoot("runtime")` opens a nested pinned `*os.Root` whose `runtimeRoot.Name()` in Go 1.24 on Windows is the relative string `"runtime"`, not the verified absolute `runtimeDir` (`filepath.Join(storageRoot, "runtime")`) assigned at line 136 and verified at lines 176–192. Subsequent calls in `openMCPDaemonLockFile` (lines 222, 243), `readMCPDaemonInfo` (lines 270, 275), and `writeMCPDaemonSidecar` (line 314) construct `filepath.Join(runtimeRoot.Name(), ...)` (`"runtime\mcp-daemon.lock"` and `"runtime\mcp-daemon.json"`) and pass that relative path to `rejectLink` -> `core.IsSymlinkOrReparsePoint` -> `windows.GetFileAttributes` (`internal/core/workspace_reparse_windows.go:19`), which resolves against process CWD and fails with `ERROR_PATH_NOT_FOUND` whenever CWD lacks a `runtime\` directory.
   * For the same reason (and as a deliberate, traceable companion carry-over from the same root cause without widening `U1b`), `U4` (`211.004-T`) on POSIX must not compare `filepath.Dir(endpoint) == runtimeRoot.Name()`, because `runtimeRoot.Name()` is `"runtime"` whenever opened via `storageHandle.OpenRoot("runtime")`; `U4` must verify upfront `filepath.Clean(filepath.Dir(endpoint)) == filepath.Clean(runtimeDir)` equality and handle-relative `runtimeRoot.Lstat(leafName)` identity per `docs/compound/2026-10-27-pin-the-handle-not-the-path.md`.
6. **Post-`U1b` Wave 1 Unfiltered Full-Suite Convergence Blockers Under Workspace-Contained `TEMP` (`60D799D9` / `086-DL`, `19D55778` / `087-DL`, and `96F1DFFD` / `086-DL`)**:
   * After `U1b` (`211.008-T`, commit `834972ab9108321c79c155022c5daaa9a9857d7f`) passed its 4 non-skippable security refusals and cleared `./internal/cli` unfiltered (`324.828s`), the Wave 1 Step 4.6 unfiltered full-suite gate (`go test -timeout=30m ./...`, captured in `bin/tmp/1793937249660-copilot-tool-output-7128-71ef1bbe-f07c-4954-a037-f7d919acbe22.txt` and diagnosed in `docs/memory/2026-11-06/212-S-U1b-green-unrelated-convergence-blockers.md`) under workspace-contained `TEMP` (`C:\Source\GitHub\backlogit\bin\_authoringtmp`) surfaced:
     * **Family 1A (`60D799D9` — Narrow Production Local-`.git`-Directory Boundary Bug)**: `TestPlanArtifactMove_StrategyDetection/empty_git_directory_fails_closed` (`internal/core/archive_git_test.go:127`) and `TestInGitWorktreeBounded` subcase `(e)` (`internal/core/shipment_gate_ancestry_test.go:152`) create an empty `.git` directory at the workspace root (`os.Mkdir(filepath.Join(root, ".git"), 0o755)`) and assert fail-closed error return. Both `planArtifactMove` (`internal/core/archive.go:843-855`) and `(*Workspace).inGitWorktreeBounded` (`internal/core/shipment_gate.go:180-214`) check for a present-but-broken `.git` directory **only on the `err != nil` (exit 128) branch**. Because Git (`setup.c: is_git_directory`) ignores an empty `.git` directory (missing `HEAD`/`refs`/`objects`) and continues walking parent directories, whenever an ancestor Git repository exists above `workspaceRoot` (nested workspaces in production or contained `TEMP` in tests), `git rev-parse` exits `0` with the ancestor's worktree root, bypassing the `err != nil` guard and failing open (`U1c` / `211.009-T`).
     * **Family 1B (`60D799D9` — Non-Hermetic "No-Usable-Repository" Test Fixtures)**: `TestInGitWorktreeBounded` subcase `(b)` (`shipment_gate_ancestry_test.go:117`), `TestHeadSHABounded` (`shipment_gate_headdrift_test.go:76`), `TestRepairShipmentMemberEvidence_NoRepoHead_Errors` (`shipment_repair_test.go:248`), and `TestU163_002_ExecGitRequiresARepositoryDirectory` (`internal/policyengine/u163_002_record_test.go:215`) use plain `t.TempDir()` directories with no local `.git` boundary, so ordinary Git discovers the outer repository (`HEAD` `834972a`) while production runners (`gate.MinimalEnv`, `isGitOverrideEnv`, `policyengine.gitEnv`) rightly strip ambient `GIT_CEILING_DIRECTORIES` (`U1c` / `211.009-T` for `TestInGitWorktreeBounded` subcase `(b)` via `writeFakeGit`, and `U1d` / `211.010-T` for the remaining 3 fixtures via owned invalid `.git` files / `initGitRepoNoCommits`).
     * **Family 2 (`19D55778` — Shipment Lifecycle / Doctor Fixture Member-Completion Sequencing)**: `TestDoctor_IgnoresIntentionalOrphans` (`internal/core/025_doctor_harness_test.go:98`) and `TestShipmentWorkflow_ShipmentReleaseCleanup` (`tests/integration/shipment_workflow_test.go:199`) call `ClaimShipment` (activating `releasedTask` `001.001-T`) and immediately call `ShipShipment` without completing `001.001-T`. Under contained `TEMP`, the default `ws.GateBroker` (`enabled: auto`) resolves the outer checkout's `HEAD`/`origin/HEAD` and enforces `validateMemberGateEvidence` (`internal/core/shipment_gate.go:750-754`), refusing the active member (`U1e` / `211.011-T`, bounded to those 2 files).
     * **Family 3 (`96F1DFFD` — Initially Recorded Pending-Verification No-Code Diagnostic Disposition in `086-DL`, Subsequently Adjudicated in `088-DL` as Wave-1 Prerequisite `U1f` / `211.012-T` After `M11` Reproduction and Public-API Core Proof, in `089-DL` as Wave-3 Recovery Prerequisite `U1g` / `211.013-T`, in `089-DL` Wave 11 as Bounded Exponential Backoff Retry `U1i` / `211.020-T` and `U1j` / `211.021-T`, and in `089-DL` Wave 12 as `U6` / `211.006-T` Sidecar Replacement Retry + `U1k` / `211.022-T` Gate Subprocess Isolation & Probe Caching)**: After `U1c`–`U1e` completed (`HEAD` `2c87ae6fdf64480ab6767e9015cae51f24abc2e6`), the `M11` 50-package full suite (`3,871` top-level passes, `9` top-level failures, `0` timeouts) and short-`TEMP` (`bin/_qf4781986`, 41 chars) comparison cleared `7` of `8` selected Git path-length failures, but `TestU2_ReconstituteShipmentsAtomicExecutionAndRollback/concurrent_associate_commit_and_post_hook_isolation` (`internal/core/shipment_reconstitute_test.go:499`) failed in both runs with `associate commit step 1 (frontmatter scalar): move temp over destination: backlogit: durable write not applied: Access is denied.`. Under the operator's verbatim authorization (`"Fourth iteration approved."` at `2026-11-06T16:57:46.238Z`, authorizing 1 additional current-wave convergence cycle without resetting the 3 prior failures), Ship's controlled public-API Core proof (`bin/_c4e7a91b2/evidence2.json`, `PID 61948`, exit `0`) and official Microsoft `FILE_RENAME_INFORMATION` / `MoveFileExW` documentation proved that legacy `MoveFileExW` (`MOVEFILE_REPLACE_EXISTING | MOVEFILE_WRITE_THROUGH`, flags `9`) denies replacement with `syscall.Errno(5)` (`ErrWriteNotApplied`) whenever any target handle is held open—both ordinary Go `os.Open` (`share 3`) and explicit `CreateFileW` (`share 7` with `FILE_SHARE_DELETE`)—and succeeds when the reader handle is closed before `AssociateCommit`. Source trace of `internal/core/shipment_reconstitute_exec.go:319-345` proved that `executeReconstitution` runs its preliminary `peek, err := planReconstitution(ctx, ws, opts)` (which opens `.md` files via `readShipmentFromDiskReadOnly`, `findArtifact`, and `scanCanonicalArtifacts` -> `parseFile` -> `os.ReadFile`) **before** acquiring `lockActiveStash(ws)`, `lockShipmentLifecycleGlobal(ctx, ws)`, and `lockShipmentMembership(ctx, ws, id)`, overlapping `AssociateCommit`'s `MoveFileExW` replacement under `lockShipmentLifecycleGlobal` (`D12`, `088-DL`, `U1f` / `211.012-T`). Subsequently, after all `19` tasks in `M19` reached `done` (`HEAD` `ddc57037d321185246652c3f1f93075622bd488f`), the Wave 10 Ordinal 8 unfiltered full-suite run (`bin/_rrc7e4b298/wave10-ordinal8/result.json`, recorded in `docs/memory/2026-11-09/212-S-wave10-ordinal8-convergence-blocked.md`) failed in `internal/core` on two transient Windows `Access is denied` collisions: `TestUR1B_UnblockIsTargetAwareAndPreservesResumptionEvidence/active_restores_exact_snapshot` while replacing a shipment-operation journal via `renameShipmentOpsWindowsChild` (`NtSetInformationFile` `FileRenameInformation` in `internal/core/shipment_ops_windows.go:182-215`) and `TestP021ClaimSerialization_RepeatedMembershipAndGenericWritersContend/iteration_01` in a generic writer replacing an artifact via `atomicReplace` (`MoveFileExW` in `internal/atomicfile/atomicfile_windows.go:50-56`). Under the operator's explicit 2026-11-09 and `2026-11-09T21:13:12Z` (`"Authorized."`) directives (`"ok, this means that we need to implement a bounded, exponential backoff retry mechanism for all of these file operations where file I/O contention in concurrent operations could easily create operational collisions."`), Wave 11 adds `U1i` (`211.020-T` in `internal/atomicfile/atomicfile_windows.go`) and `U1j` (`211.021-T` in `internal/core/shipment_ops_windows.go`) implementing bounded exponential backoff retry (`6` retries / `7` total attempts, `1ms, 2ms, 4ms, 8ms, 16ms, 32ms` = `63ms` max total wait) strictly for transient Windows `ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED`, `ERROR_SHARING_VIOLATION`, and `STATUS_DELETE_PENDING` while skipping backoff retry immediately (`0` retries, `0` sleeps on attempt `1`) when the destination/target path carries `windows.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_READONLY` or `windows.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY` (`D17`, `089-DL`). Finally, in Wave 11 **Ordinal 10** (`bin/_rr9501da8a/wave11-ordinal10/result.json`, SHA-256 `4f0915989d1a4a56b549264917ad47d5f9954b4e89e9729d2c3450b6a4c77c70`, recorded in `docs/memory/2026-11-09/212-S-wave11-ordinal10-convergence-blocked.md` at `HEAD` `b6fdacfd2a017d49c058de6f0d5fcc30e388d1a1`), `U1i`/`U1j` eliminated all Windows `Access is denied` / `Sharing violation` failures across `2,849` top-level passes, leaving two remaining convergence blockers: (1) `TestU6_PrewarmAndLazyAutoStartColdRaceConvergence` in `internal/cli` failed with `runtime/mcp-daemon.json changed while opening` when `readMCPDaemonInfoAt` observed `!os.SameFile(opened, linked)` at the instant `RunMCPDaemon` replaced the lock-only or stale `mcp-daemon.json` sidecar with the endpoint-ready regular file, and (2) `internal/core` timed out at `1,802.332s` because under workspace-contained `TEMP` (`bin/_t1`), `ExecGitRunner.Verify` (`internal/core/gate/baseref.go:118`) walked up out of non-git `t.TempDir()` workspaces into the outer repository checkout (`C:\Source\GitHub\backlogit\.git`) and together with uncached `ExecVersionRunner.Version` (`internal/core/gate/probe.go:108`) spawned ~`25 minutes` of unintended Python `autoharness` subprocesses across `1,195` tests. Under the operator's explicit `"Proceed."` directive (`2026-11-09T23:24:15Z`), `D18` (`089-DL`) resolves both blockers via the same-contract `U6` (`211.006-T`) sidecar replacement retry in `internal/cli/mcp_daemon.go` & `internal/cli/mcp_session_shim_test.go`, Wave 12 prerequisite `U1k` (`211.022-T` in `internal/core/gate/{baseref.go,probe.go,probe_test.go}`), and Post-Wave-12 **Ordinal ## Decisions and Rationale (`D1`–`D18` Resolved)

1. **`D1` — FIFO Go Channel Coordinator Parameters, Synchronous-Frame Turn Ownership, and External Writer Boundary (`R7`–`R9`, `R12`)**:
   * *Decision*: Implement `OperationCoordinator` in `internal/mcp/coordinator.go` with `DefaultMutationQueueCapacity = 32`, `DefaultMutationAdmissionTimeout = 30 * time.Second` (matching SQLite `busy_timeout(30000)`), `DefaultMaxConcurrentReads = 4` (matching `db.Open` `SetMaxOpenConns(4)` in `internal/db/connection.go:75`), and `DefaultReadAdmissionTimeout = 15 * time.Second`.
   * *Synchronous-frame turn handoff (`R9`, `timeout-goroutine-lock-ownership-go-2026-07-01.md`)*: Each `OpMutating` call enqueues a `*mutationWaiter` carrying an unbuffered rendezvous channel `grant chan chan struct{}` and cancellation signal `abandoned chan struct{}` into the bounded `mutationQueue` (`cap = 32`). When the sequential coordinator loop reaches the waiter, it selects between `waiter.grant <- done` and `<-waiter.abandoned`. The caller waits in its **synchronous execution frame** for `releaseTurn := <-waiter.grant` vs `<-ctx.Done()` vs `<-admissionTimer.C`, registers `defer close(releaseTurn)` in that synchronous frame, and executes the tool handler directly in the caller goroutine (never spawning a timeout goroutine that could strand locks). `(*OperationCoordinator).Close()` signals shutdown by closing an internal `closed chan struct{}` (never closing `mutationQueue` directly, avoiding `send on closed channel` panics).
    * *Exclusive consistency barrier (`R8`)*: `OpExclusiveBarrier` (`backlogit_sync_index`) first acquires the FIFO mutation turn and then acquires all `4` tokens from `readTokens` (tracking `acquiredCount` and releasing all `acquiredCount` tokens via synchronous-frame `defer` even if `ctx.Done()` or admission timeout aborts after acquiring 1–3 tokens) before running `Rehydrate`. Similarly, when `s.Workspace == nil` on entry to a tool call in `internal/mcp/server.go`, lazy workspace initialization (`s.ensureWorkspace`) is gated under `s.workspaceMu` (and `s.backlogitDir()` locks `s.workspaceMu`) before handler dispatch so `s.Workspace`, `s.templateSvc`, `s.Events`, `s.Telemetry`, and `s.HookEvents` never race with concurrent readers. Because only the active mutation holder ever acquires all 4 read tokens and readers never acquire the mutation turn, lock ordering is strictly `MutationTurn -> ReadTokens` (deadlock-free).
   * *External writer boundary (`R7`, `R12`)*: `OperationCoordinator` schedules `backlogit` runtime-managed MCP operations only. One-shot CLI commands and external editors continue to coordinate through `.backlogit/.locks/` advisory file locks (`shipment-lifecycle-global -> A -> C -> B`) and SQLite WAL `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` + `busy_timeout(30000)`.
2. **`D2` — Deterministic Platform IPC Endpoint Naming and Workspace-Contained POSIX `sun_path` Handling (`R16`, `A4`, Constitution Principle IV)**:
   * *Decision*: Resolve `canonicalStorageRoot` and expected `runtimeDir = filepath.Join(storageRoot, "runtime")` once up front (`EvalSymlinks`-resolved, cleaned absolute `storageRoot`, lowercased on Windows — never calling `EvalSymlinks` or re-checking directory identity by path inside an open `*os.Root` pin window per `docs/compound/2026-10-27-pin-the-handle-not-the-path.md`), use handle-relative `runtimeRoot.Lstat`/`Open`/`Remove` only, bind opened files with `os.SameFile(opened, linked)` against handle-relative `Lstat`, and compute `hexHash16 = hex(sha256(canonicalStorageRoot)[:8])`.
   * *Windows (`go:build windows`)*: Named Pipe endpoint `\\.\pipe\backlogit-mcp-<hexHash16>`, created via `golang.org/x/sys/windows` `CreateNamedPipe` with `PIPE_ACCESS_DUPLEX | FILE_FLAG_OVERLAPPED`, `PIPE_REJECT_REMOTE_CLIENTS`, and a security descriptor (`SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES`) whose DACL grants access exclusively to the current process owner SID (`TOKEN_USER`, SDDL `D:P(A;;GA;;;<UserSID>)`).
   * *POSIX (`go:build !windows` — strictly workspace-contained, zero `/tmp` / `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR` / `$HOME` fallback)*: Per `R16` and Constitution Principle IV (`CLI Workspace Containment (NON-NEGOTIABLE)`), no socket or directory is ever created outside `<storage_root>/runtime/`. Primary Unix Domain Socket path is `primaryPath = filepath.Join(runtimeDir, "mcp.sock")` with mode `0600` inside `runtimeRoot` (`*os.Root`). When `len(primaryPath) > 100` (approaching the 104/108-byte `sockaddr_un.sun_path` kernel limit), `DeriveMCPDaemonEndpoint` falls back to the short workspace-contained socket leaf `shortPath = filepath.Join(runtimeDir, "s")` (still inside `<storage_root>/runtime/` and opened/removed relative to `runtimeRoot` `*os.Root` with mode `0600`) if `len(shortPath) <= 100`. When even `len(shortPath) > 100`, `DeriveMCPDaemonEndpoint` and `ListenMCPDaemonIPC` deterministically refuse with a wrapped `corerrors.ErrValidation` (`"unix domain socket path %q (%d bytes) exceeds 100-byte sun_path limit; move the workspace to a shorter path or set BACKLOGIT_WORKSPACE_DIR to a shorter workspace-contained storage path"`) and never fall back to `/tmp`, `$TMPDIR`, `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR`, or `$HOME`.
3. **`D3` — Idle Auto-Shutdown Grace Period and Prewarm Idle Window (`R11`, `R13`, `R14`)**:
   * *Decision*: Set `DefaultDaemonIdleTimeout = 15 * time.Minute` (overridable via `--idle-timeout` or `BACKLOGIT_MCP_IDLE_TIMEOUT`). The timer starts at daemon boot (covering unattached `backlogit mcp prewarm` runs) and stops whenever `active_sessions > 0`. When the last client session disconnects (`active_sessions` drops to `0`), the timer resets to `idleTimeout`; when it expires with `active_sessions == 0`, the daemon closes the IPC listener, removes `mcp-daemon.json`, releases `mcp-daemon.lock`, closes `Workspace`, and exits `0`.
4. **`D4` — Binary Version-Skew and Wrong-Workspace Refusal Recovery UX (`R15`, `SC7`, `SC8`)**:
   * *Decision*: `VerifyMCPDaemonAttachment` checks both `mcp-daemon.json` and the IPC handshake (`backlogit-mcp-ipc/v1`). Wrong-workspace/storage mismatches return `corerrors.ErrDaemonWorkspaceMismatch` (`retryable: false`) and never replace a foreign daemon. Binary path or version/commit mismatches return `corerrors.ErrDaemonVersionMismatch` with the daemon PID, running vs expected version/binary path, `active_sessions`, and explicit CLI remediation (`backlogit mcp stop` or `backlogit mcp --replace`), replacing only when `--replace` is explicitly passed. Missing workspace-pinned binary returns `corerrors.ErrDaemonBinaryMissing` with zero global `PATH` fallback.
5. **`D5` — Non-Exclusive Status and Handshake Probing (`R3`, `A045DBA4`)**:
   * *Decision*: `InspectMCPDaemonStatus`, `StopMCPDaemon` (when no daemon is running), and shim connect probes first resolve `lockPath` via `resolveMCPDaemonLeafPath(runtimeRoot, runtimeDir, mcpDaemonLockName)` and perform a lock-free `runtimeRoot.Lstat(mcpDaemonLockName)` + `rejectLink(info, lockPath)` check (preserving `TestU2_RejectsSymlinkOrReparseRuntimeDir/lock_leaf_symlink` and `TestU1b_NestedRuntimeRootUsesCanonicalAbsolutePathForReparseValidation`), then read `"mcp-daemon.json"` via `readMCPDaemonInfoAt(runtimeRoot, runtimeDir)` (which resolves `sidecarPath` via `resolveMCPDaemonLeafPath(runtimeRoot, runtimeDir, mcpDaemonSidecarName)` on a no-follow read handle relative to `runtimeRoot`, **zero lock on `mcp-daemon.lock`**) and check `isVerifiedProcessAlive(info.PID, info.ProcessStartTime, info.ExecutablePath)` plus a non-disruptive `500ms` IPC `"status"` ping (when `info.Endpoint != ""`). If `"mcp-daemon.json"` is absent (`os.ErrNotExist`), `InspectMCPDaemonStatus` returns `Running: false` without locking `mcp-daemon.lock`. Only when `"mcp-daemon.json"` exists AND `isVerifiedProcessAlive` proves the recorded process is dead does `InspectMCPDaemonStatus` acquire `openMCPDaemonLockFile(runtimeRoot, runtimeDir)` to remove the stale sidecar under lock.
6. **`D6` — `start.ps1` Prewarm Command Contract and CLI Surface (`R11`, `R14`, `A5`)**:
   * *Decision*: Add `backlogit mcp daemon [--replace] [--idle-timeout <dur>]` and `backlogit mcp prewarm [--replace] [--idle-timeout <dur>] [--format text|json]` under `backlogit mcp`, plus `--toolchain` and `--strict` flags on `backlogit mcp status` (preserving `newMCPCommand.Short` and `newMCPStatusCommand.Short` so `cmd/gen-docs` updates only the target CLI reference files). Verify in `U6` (`TestU6_MissingPinnedBinaryRefusalAndCLIWiring`) that `backlogit mcp daemon` and `backlogit mcp prewarm` are registered on `NewRootCommand()` as intentional Class-E CLI-only lifecycle commands unpaired to any MCP tool (keeping existing `TestRegistryParity_*` in `internal/cli/registry_parity_test.go` green throughout both `U5` and `U6`) and regenerate `docs/cli-reference/` via `go run ./cmd/gen-docs docs/cli-reference` in `U7` and `U13`.
7. **`D7` — External Policy Authority Boundary Note for Future Hostile-Agent / OS-Sandbox Phase (`R18`, `R23`, `A7`)**:
   * *Decision*: Document in `docs/design-docs/2026-11-04-multi-session-mcp-runtime-and-workspace-toolchain.md` and `ToolchainVerificationReport` (`containment_level: "level-1-workspace-local-pinned"`, `os_container_sandbox_deferred: true`) that workspace-writable files (`.mcp.json`, `start.ps1`, `.backlogit/toolchain.yaml`) guard against accidental `PATH` drift and cross-workspace bleed, whereas defending against a hostile local process running under the same OS user requires an out-of-workspace policy authority and Level 2 OS/container sandboxing (deferred per `Q3`).
8. **`D8` — SDK (`mcp-go v0.27.0`) Progress-Notification Verification (`R10`, `A3`)**:
   * *Decision*: Verified in `github.com/mark3labs/mcp-go v0.27.0` (`mcp.CallToolRequest.Params.Meta.ProgressToken` and `MCPServer.SendNotificationToClient`). When `req.Params.Meta != nil && req.Params.Meta.ProgressToken != nil`, `OperationCoordinator` emits standard `notifications/progress` messages (`progressToken`, `progress`, `progressTotal`, `message`) to the calling session's `NotificationChannel()` on queue wait, execution start, and completion without changing synchronous `tools/call` response semantics.

    * *Evidence vs. Attribution Uncertainty*:

    * *Decision (`089-DL` Route A Extended under Verbatim Operator Directives at 2026-11-09: `"ok, this means that we need to implement a bounded, exponential backoff retry mechanism for all of these file operations where file I/O contention in concurrent operations could easily create operational collisions."` and `2026-11-09T21:13:12Z`: `"Authorized."`)*:
      * Because both tasks modify **existing unexported functions** (`atomicReplace` in `internal/atomicfile/atomicfile_windows.go`, and `renameShipmentOpsWindowsChild` / `removeShipmentOperationJournalTempFile` / `openShipmentOpsWindowsChild` in `internal/core/shipment_ops_windows.go`) and introduce **no new exported symbols**, `harness-architect` scaffolds behavioral RED tests (`TestU1i_*` and `TestU1j_*`) directly in Wave 11 without separate `go/ast` source-shape declaration tasks.
      * Add two width-isolated Wave 11 tasks under Feature `211-F` in Shipment `212-S`, both depending on `211.007-T` (`U7`, which is `done` at `e96211234be6dbbf55f0db48dfe72e205f48ed4d`) so `ready_11 = {211.020-T, 211.021-T}` with 100% disjoint file scopes, and tune their backoff and permanent-denial fast-fail rules:
        1. **`U1i` (`211.020-T`, `code`, `status: done`, `priority: high`, `size: XS`, `complexity: low`, `labels: code,track-b,windows,atomicfile,96f1dffd,u1i,harness-ready`, `dependencies: [211.007-T]`, `2` files: `internal/atomicfile/atomicfile_windows.go`, `internal/atomicfile/atomicfile_windows_test.go`)**: In `internal/atomicfile/atomicfile_windows.go`, add unexported sleep seam `var atomicReplaceSleep = time.Sleep` (`atomicReplaceMaxRetries = 6`, `atomicReplaceInitialBackoff = 1 * time.Millisecond`) and helper `isTransientWindowsReplaceError(err error) bool` (`errors.Is(err, windows.ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED) || errors.Is(err, windows.ERROR_SHARING_VIOLATION)`). Skip backoff retry immediately (fail on attempt `1` with `0` retries and `0` sleeps) when the destination/target path carries `windows.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_READONLY` or `windows.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY` (so negative tests in `internal/core` that intentionally make a target file read-only `0o400` or a directory fail in `0ms` instead of sleeping). In `atomicReplace(tmpName, path string, durable bool) error`, retry `moveFileEx(tmpName, path, moveFileExFlags(durable))` up to `6` times (`7` total attempts) with initial delay `1 * time.Millisecond`, doubling each retry (`1ms, 2ms, 4ms, 8ms, 16ms, 32ms` = `63ms` max total wait, covering the 1–30ms Windows minifilter window while cutting worst-case backoff sleep by 10x from `635ms` to `63ms`) **only** when `isTransientWindowsReplaceError(err)` is true and destination path does not carry `FILE_ATTRIBUTE_READONLY` or `FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY`. Non-transient errors (including generic `errors.New(...)` used by `TestAtomicReplace_FailureReturnsNotApplied` and `TestWriteFileAtomic_ReplaceFailureLeavesOriginalIntact`) and permanent attribute denials (`FILE_ATTRIBUTE_READONLY` / `FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY`) fail immediately on attempt `1` with `0` retries and `0` sleeps; if transient errors persist past the `7` total attempts, return the wrapped `blerrors.ErrWriteNotApplied` error and leave the destination file intact. Scoped verification: `go test -timeout=30m -count=1 -race -run '^(TestU1i_|TestAtomicReplace_|TestWriteFileAtomic_|TestMoveFileExFlags_)' ./internal/atomicfile`.
        2. **`U1j` (`211.021-T`, `code`, `status: done`, `priority: high`, `size: XS`, `complexity: low`, `labels: code,track-b,core,windows,96f1dffd,u1j,harness-ready`, `dependencies: [211.007-T]`, `2` files: `internal/core/shipment_ops_windows.go`, `internal/core/shipment_ops_security_windows_test.go`)**: In `internal/core/shipment_ops_windows.go`, add unexported seams `var shipmentOpsNtRenameFn = windows.NtSetInformationFile`, `var shipmentOpsSetDispositionFn = windows.SetFileInformationByHandle`, and `var shipmentOpsRetrySleep = time.Sleep`. Skip backoff retry immediately (fail on attempt `1` with `0` retries and `0` sleeps) when the destination/target path carries `windows.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_READONLY` or `windows.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY` (so negative tests in `internal/core` that intentionally make a target file read-only `0o400` or a directory fail in `0ms` instead of sleeping). In `renameShipmentOpsWindowsChild` and `removeShipmentOperationJournalTempFile` (and `openShipmentOpsWindowsChild` when opening an existing journal or temp file hits transient `STATUS_SHARING_VIOLATION` / `STATUS_DELETE_PENDING` / `STATUS_ACCESS_DENIED` on a regular non-read-only file), retry up to `6` times (`7` total attempts) with initial delay `1 * time.Millisecond` doubling each retry (`1ms, 2ms, 4ms, 8ms, 16ms, 32ms` = `63ms` max total wait) when the normalized Windows error (`shipmentOpsWindowsError(err)`) is `windows.ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED`, `windows.ERROR_SHARING_VIOLATION`, or `windows.STATUS_DELETE_PENDING` (excluding `STATUS_FILE_IS_A_DIRECTORY` and destination/target paths carrying `FILE_ATTRIBUTE_READONLY` or `FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY`), while preserving all existing symlink/reparse-point and directory-handle binding security checks (`validateShipmentOpsWindowsFileHandle` returning `blerrors.ErrValidation` is never retried). Scoped verification: `go test -timeout=30m -count=1 -race -run '^(TestU1j_|TestUR1B_|TestP021ClaimSerialization_|TestShipmentOpsJournal|TestWriteShipmentLifecycleJournal_|TestNewWorkspace_Rejects)' ./internal/core`.
      * *`M19 -> M21` (`S20 -> S22`) Refreeze and Ordinal 10 Convergence Authorization (`NOFEATURECOMMIT`)*: Keep all `21` task members (`211.001-T`..`211.021-T`) as `done`/archived in `terminal_success` (`open_red_deliverables = ∅`), leave `checkpoint-20261109-204413.json` and `checkpoint-20261106-210317.json` untouched on disk during Stage's turn, retain historical counters (`8` prior failed full-suite runs, `failed_count: 8`), and under the operator's explicit `"Authorized."` (`2026-11-09T21:13:12Z`) directive authorize **Ordinal 10** (`ordinal: 10`, `failed_count: 8`, `budget_seconds: 2700`, `46` first-party packages) unfiltered full-suite convergence (`go test -count=1 -p=1 -timeout=30m -json ./...`) at Wave 11 Step 4.6 after the `U1i`/`U1j` tuning commit lands on `212-S`.

      4. **Wave 12 Task `211.022-T` (`U1k`, Preserving `status: done`, `20` Affected Go Files Total) & Docline Corpus Follow-Up Under `211-F` / `212-S`**:

## Constraints and Harness-Exempt Contract

* **2-Hour Rule and Width Isolation**: Every implementation unit (`U1`, `U1b`–`U1j`, `U2`, `U2i`, `U3a`, `U4a`, `U5a`, `U3`–`U13`, with `U2j` and `U1k` carrying their documented 6-file coupled-contract exceptions) touches **at most 3 files**, **at most 4 functions**, **at most 3 test scenarios**, and a **single skill domain** (`code`, `tests`, or `docs`).
* **No hardcoded `.backlogit` literals in Go packages**: Per `TestWorkspaceLiteralGuard_NoHardcodedLegacyStorageRoot` (`internal/core/workspace_literal_guard_test.go`), `internal/cli`, `internal/mcp`, and `internal/toolchain` MUST resolve workspace storage roots via `core.ResolveStorageRoot`, `core.WorkspaceStorageRoot`, and `core.WorkspaceRootCandidates()`.
* **P-002.6 Intra-Shipment Task Dependency Rule (`deps(t) ⊆ M`)**: Every task-to-task `blocks` dependency edge (`item_deps` on a task `t`) targets a task inside the **same** shipment's task set `M`. Cross-shipment dependencies are recorded as shipment-to-shipment `blocks` edges (`214-S` blocked by `212-S` and `213-S` via `AddShipmentBlock`) and feature-to-feature `blocks` edges (`213-F` blocked by `211-F` and `212-F`).
* **Closed P-002.1 `harness-exempt` set across this plan**:
  * Shipment `212-S` (`211-F`, Track B): `["211.007-T"]` (`U7`, `docs-only` CLI reference regeneration). All other 21 tasks (`211.001-T`..`211.006-T`, `211.008-T`..`211.022-T`) are harness-required (non-exempt; all 21 non-exempt tasks `211.001-T`..`211.006-T` and `211.008-T`..`211.022-T` are `done`/archived with verified `harness-ready`).
  * Shipment `213-S` (`212-F`, Track A): `["212.003-T"]` (`U10`, `docs-only` architecture & design documentation). All other tasks (`212.001-T`..`212.002-T`) are `harness-ready`.
  * Shipment `214-S` (`213-F`, Track C): `["213.003-T"]` (`U13`, `docs-only` CLI reference + configuration documentation). All other tasks (`213.001-T`..`213.002-T`) are `harness-ready`.

#### U1b: Repair Windows Go 1.24 Nested `os.Root` Runtime Path Validation for Daemon Lock and Sidecar (`code`) — `211.008-T`

* **Domain**: `code` (executed under **`careful` + `freeze-scope` safety modes** per Constitution Principle VIII, bounding edits strictly to the 2 declared files and 4 core leaf-validation functions in `internal/cli/mcp_daemon.go`).

  * **First (Genuine RED phase, scaffolded by `harness-architect` in `internal/cli/mcp_daemon_lock_test.go` with zero production files modified — `M3` Hardened Contract)**:
    1. Add sequential test `TestU1b_NestedRuntimeRootUsesCanonicalAbsolutePathForReparseValidation` in `internal/cli/mcp_daemon_lock_test.go` (**no `t.Parallel()`** because it changes process CWD via `t.Chdir`; create all `t.TempDir()` directories before calling `t.Chdir` so `t.Cleanup` restores CWD before temp directory removal on Windows).
    2. **Must compile cleanly on all platforms (`Windows` and `POSIX`, with zero `golang.org/x/sys/windows` import in untagged `mcp_daemon_lock_test.go`) against existing `internal/cli/mcp_daemon.go` and fail on assertions (`Red Phase: CONFIRMED` on Windows; cross-platform regression guard on POSIX)**: Call existing exported production entrypoints (`AcquireMCPDaemonLock`, `InspectMCPDaemonStatus`, `StopMCPDaemon`) with default options—never calling an undefined helper that would fail at compile time instead of assertion time.
    3. **Subcase 1 (`cwd_without_runtime_and_decoy_isolation`)**: Create workspace `root` via `u2Workspace(t)` and `unrelatedCWD := t.TempDir()`, then `t.Chdir(unrelatedCWD)` where `unrelatedCWD` initially contains no `runtime/` directory (and then contains a benign decoy `unrelatedCWD/runtime/mcp-daemon.lock` and `unrelatedCWD/runtime/mcp-daemon.json` sentinel). Invoke `AcquireMCPDaemonLock(t.Context(), root, false)`, `InspectMCPDaemonStatus(root)`, `release()`, and `StopMCPDaemon(t.Context(), root)` with a stale sidecar. Assert all operations succeed (`require.NoError`) and never read or mutate the decoy files under `unrelatedCWD/runtime/`. (On unpatched `mcp_daemon.go` on Windows, this compiles cleanly and fails on assertions with `inspect runtime\mcp-daemon.lock: get file attributes runtime\mcp-daemon.lock: The system cannot find the path specified.`).
    4. **Subcase 2 (`malicious_lock_and_sidecar_leaf_refusal_no_skip`)**: While `t.Chdir(unrelatedCWD)` is active (and `unrelatedCWD/runtime/` holds benign regular decoy files so a CWD-relative check would inspect the wrong object), test both a malicious **lock leaf** (`<storage_root>/runtime/mcp-daemon.lock`) and a malicious **sidecar leaf** (`<storage_root>/runtime/mcp-daemon.json`):
       * **Cross-platform non-skippable fixture creation (no `golang.org/x/sys/windows` import needed)**: On Windows (`runtime.GOOS == "windows"`), create a directory junction at `leafPath` (`exec.CommandContext(t.Context(), "cmd", "/c", "mklink", "/J", leafPath, externalTargetDir).CombinedOutput()`) as the primary non-privileged reparse fixture, and assert via standard library `os.Lstat(leafPath)` (`info.Mode()&os.ModeSymlink == 0`) plus `core.IsSymlinkOrReparsePoint(info, leafPath)` (already imported in `mcp_daemon_lock_test.go` via `internal/core`) that `isReparse == true` while `ModeSymlink == 0`, guaranteeing that `core.IsSymlinkOrReparsePoint` executes the path-based `windows.GetFileAttributes` branch rather than short-circuiting on `ModeSymlink`! On POSIX (`runtime.GOOS != "windows"`), create the leaf fixture via `os.Symlink(externalTarget, leafPath)`. **Never call `t.Skip` or `t.Skipf`**: require fixture creation via `require.NoError(t, err, "required reparse/symlink fixture unavailable")` without altering global system configuration.
       * **Lock-leaf refusal (`2` refusals)**: With the malicious reparse/symlink fixture at `<storage_root>/runtime/mcp-daemon.lock`, call `AcquireMCPDaemonLock(t.Context(), root, false)` and `InspectMCPDaemonStatus(root)`. Assert `errors.Is(err, corerrors.ErrValidation)` on both calls and verify the external target directory is untouched.
       * **Sidecar-leaf refusal while lock is held (`2` refusals)**: Because `InspectMCPDaemonStatus` and `AcquireMCPDaemonLock` (on second-handle contention via per-open-file-description `tryLockFile` in `mcp_daemon.go:370-373, 445-452`) read the sidecar when `mcp-daemon.lock` is held and return `readErr` directly when `errors.Is(readErr, corerrors.ErrValidation)` (winning over `ErrDaemonAlreadyRunning`), first acquire and hold the lock on a fresh workspace via `release, err := AcquireMCPDaemonLock(t.Context(), root2, false)` (registering `t.Cleanup(func() { if rmErr := os.Remove(filepath.Join(runtimeDir2, u2SidecarName)); rmErr != nil && !errors.Is(rmErr, os.ErrNotExist) { t.Errorf("cleanup sidecar fixture: %v", rmErr) }; assert.NoError(t, release()) })` so the malicious fixture is cleaned up with non-`ErrNotExist` errors reported and `release()`'s return error is explicitly asserted rather than discarded), remove `<storage_root>/runtime/mcp-daemon.json`, place the malicious reparse/symlink fixture at `<storage_root>/runtime/mcp-daemon.json` targeting an external directory/file with untouched sentinel content, and while the lock is held call `InspectMCPDaemonStatus(root2)` and `AcquireMCPDaemonLock(t.Context(), root2, false)`. Assert `errors.Is(err, corerrors.ErrValidation)` and `!errors.Is(err, corerrors.ErrDaemonAlreadyRunning)` on both calls, verify the external target is untouched, and assert `executedRefusals == 4` and `skippedFixtures == 0`.
  * **Second (GREEN phase, implemented by `build-feature` in `internal/cli/mcp_daemon.go` — `M3` 4-Function Core Budget & Upfront-Verified Absolute `runtimeDir` Propagation)**:
    1. Modify/add at most **4 core leaf-validation functions** in `internal/cli/mcp_daemon.go` (plus mechanical 1-line `runtimeDir` parameter threading at their call sites inside `mcp_daemon.go`, with zero edits to any file outside `internal/cli/mcp_daemon.go` and `internal/cli/mcp_daemon_lock_test.go`):
       * `resolveMCPDaemonLeafPath(runtimeRoot *os.Root, runtimeDir, leaf string) (string, error)` (new unexported helper): validates `runtimeRoot != nil`, `runtimeDir != ""`, `filepath.IsAbs(runtimeDir)`, and `leaf != "" && filepath.Base(leaf) == leaf`, and verifies handle-relatively via `runtimeRoot.Stat(".")` that the pinned root handle is a valid directory without re-statting `runtimeDir` by path inside the open pin window (`docs/compound/2026-10-27-pin-the-handle-not-the-path.md`, since `openMCPDaemonRuntimeRoot` already resolved and verified `runtimeDir` once at pin-open time); fails closed with `mcpDaemonValidationError` if `runtimeDir` is empty or relative, and returns `filepath.Join(runtimeDir, leaf), nil`. (All production paths in `mcp_daemon.go` pass the upfront-verified `runtimeDir` from `openMCPDaemonRuntimeRoot` and never inspect `runtimeRoot.Name()`.)
       * `openMCPDaemonLockFile(runtimeRoot *os.Root, runtimeDir string) (file *os.File, acquired bool, err error)`: calls `resolveMCPDaemonLeafPath(runtimeRoot, runtimeDir, mcpDaemonLockName)` to obtain `lockPath` and passes `lockPath` to `rejectLink` before and after `runtimeRoot.OpenFile`. In `mcpDaemonOptions`, rename the unused second `string` parameter of `OpenLock func(runtimeRoot *os.Root, runtimeDir string) (*os.File, bool, error)` so `defaultMCPDaemonOptions()` (remaining argument-free for `mcp_daemon_cli_test.go`) wires `OpenLock: openMCPDaemonLockFile` directly, and `acquireMCPDaemonLockWithOptions`, `InspectMCPDaemonStatus`, and `stopMCPDaemonWithOptions` (including its `reverify` closure and post-stop poll loop) pass `(runtimeRoot, runtimeDir)`.

#### U2: Implement FIFO Go Channel Mutation Coordinator, Bounded Read Limiter, Exclusive Barrier, and Progress Notifications (`code`) — `211.002-T`

* **Domain**: `code`.
* **Depends on**: `U1` (`211.001-T`), `U1b` (`211.008-T`), `U1c` (`211.009-T`), `U1d` (`211.010-T`), `U1e` (`211.011-T`), `U1f` (`211.012-T`).
* **Changes**:
  * In `internal/mcp/coordinator.go`:
    1. Implement `ClassifyToolOperation(toolName string, args map[string]any) OperationKind`:
       * Returns `OpExclusiveBarrier` for `backlogit_sync_index`.
       * Returns `OpReadOnly` for the 18 always-read-only tools in `R8` plus `backlogit_list_templates`, `backlogit_get_wit_metadata`, `backlogit_list_types`, `backlogit_poll_hook_events`, `backlogit_custody_preview`, and `backlogit_custody_verify`, as well as `backlogit_doctor` when `fix_orphans != true`, `backlogit_docs_migrate` when `apply != true`, and `backlogit_merge_sync` / `backlogit_remove_from_shipment` / `backlogit_move_shipment_item` / `backlogit_reorder_shipment` / `backlogit_reconstitute_shipment` / `backlogit_custody_offer` / `backlogit_custody_accept` when `dry_run == true`.
       * Returns `OpMutating` for all other tools (and when `fix_orphans == true`, `apply == true`, or `dry_run == false` on the conditional tools above).
    2. Implement `NewOperationCoordinator`, `(*OperationCoordinator).Execute`, and `(*OperationCoordinator).Close`:
       * `(*OperationCoordinator).Close()` signals shutdown by closing an internal `closed chan struct{}` (never closing `mutationQueue` directly, preventing `send on closed channel` panics on concurrent `Execute` calls).
       * `OpMutating` enqueues a `*mutationWaiter` (`grant chan chan struct{}`, `abandoned chan struct{}`) onto bounded `mutationQueue` (`cap = 32`). If `mutationQueue` is full (or `<-c.closed` is signaled), immediately returns a structured `BusyErrorResponse` (`Error: "server_busy"`, `Code: "mutation_queue_full"`, `Retryable: true`) wrapped via `mcplib.NewToolResultError` without mutating state.
       * Waits in the caller's synchronous frame for `releaseTurn := <-waiter.grant` vs `<-ctx.Done()` vs `<-admissionTimer.C` vs `<-c.closed`. On timeout or context cancel while queued, closes `waiter.abandoned`, drains any racing grant (`select { case r := <-waiter.grant: close(r) default: }`), and returns `BusyErrorResponse` (`Error: "server_busy"`, `Code: "mutation_admission_timeout"` or `"request_cancelled"`, `Retryable: true`).
       * On turn grant, binds `defer close(releaseTurn)` to the caller's synchronous frame (`timeout-goroutine-lock-ownership-go-2026-07-01.md`). When `kind == OpExclusiveBarrier`, acquires all `MaxConcurrentReads` (`4`) tokens from `readTokens`, tracking `acquiredCount` and registering a synchronous-frame `defer` that returns all `acquiredCount` tokens to `readTokens` even if `ctx.Done()` or `ReadAdmissionTimeout` aborts after acquiring 1–3 tokens, before invoking `next(ctx, req)`.
       * When `req.Params.Meta != nil && req.Params.Meta.ProgressToken != nil && srv != nil`, emits capability-gated `notifications/progress` updates (`queued`, `started`, `completed`) to the calling session via `srv.SendNotificationToClient(ctx, "notifications/progress", ...)` (`R10`, `D8`).
       * `OpReadOnly` acquires 1 token from `readTokens` in the caller's synchronous frame (racing `ctx.Done()` and `ReadAdmissionTimeout`, returning `BusyErrorResponse{Error: "server_busy", Code: "read_admission_timeout" | "request_cancelled", Retryable: true}` on timeout/cancel, and releasing via synchronous `defer`) and executes `next(ctx, req)` concurrently.
  * In `internal/mcp/server.go`:
    1. Add `coordinator *OperationCoordinator` to `Server`, initialize in `newServer`, and route `addTool` handlers and `InvokeTool` through `s.coordinator.Execute`.
    2. Enforce the lazy-workspace-initialization consistency barrier (`R8`) inside `internal/mcp/server.go` without touching `dynamic.go`, `hook_tools.go`, or `metadata.go`: at the `addTool` / `InvokeTool` dispatch wrapper (before `OpReadOnly` handlers run), if `s.Workspace == nil` and `dirExists(s.backlogitDir())`, invoke `s.ensureWorkspace(ctx)` (which holds `s.workspaceMu` while initializing `s.Workspace` and rebinding `s.templateSvc`, `s.Events`, `s.Telemetry`, and `s.HookEvents`), and guard `s.backlogitDir()` with `s.workspaceMu` so no reader ever races with lazy workspace initialization.
* **Affected files (`3` files)**:
  * `internal/mcp/coordinator.go`
  * `internal/mcp/server.go`
  * `internal/mcp/coordinator_test.go` (new)
* **Acceptance criteria**:
  * Scenario 1 (`TestU2_CoordinatorFIFOMutationOrderAndConcurrentReads`): 8 concurrent mutating tool calls execute strictly in FIFO admission order with zero lost writes or interleaved JSONL events (`SC3`), 4 concurrent read-only calls run in parallel without blocking behind slow mutations, and `backlogit_sync_index` (`OpExclusiveBarrier`) waits for in-flight readers to drain before rehydrating.
  * Scenario 2 (`TestU2_CoordinatorQueueFullAndCallerCancellationSafety`): saturating `mutationQueue` or cancelling a queued caller's `ctx` returns structured `BusyErrorResponse` (`retryable: true`) with zero partial mutations and immediately hands the turn to the next queued caller (`SC4`).
  * Scenario 3 (`TestU2_CoordinatorProgressNotificationsIsolatedToCallerSession`): a gated mutation with `_meta.progressToken` delivers `notifications/progress` only to the calling session's `NotificationChannel()` (`R10`, `D8`).
  * `go test -timeout=30m -count=1 -race -run '^TestU2_' ./internal/mcp` passes.
* **Execution posture**: `test-first`.

* **Changes**:
  * **First (Genuine RED phase, scaffolded by `harness-architect` in `internal/core/shipment_p1_lifecycle_remediation_test.go` with zero production files modified)**:
    1. Add sequential `TestU1g_LegacyReturnBlockedRecoverySkipsUnchangedTaskRewriteAndFailsClosedOnShipmentOrTaskDivergence` (no `t.Parallel()`, restoring `persistArtifactWriteFn` in `t.Cleanup`) with three subtests:
       * `unchanged_task_skips_physical_write_and_reconciles_sqlite_and_journal`: creates `feature`, `item`, and `shipment` (`[item.ID]`), writes a legacy `return-blocked` journal (`writeReturnBlockedJournal(ws, originalShipment, originalItem)`, where `journal.Reason == ""`), mutates only `shipment` membership on disk (`items: []`), leaves `item` on disk byte-unchanged (matching `originalItem`), seeds a stale SQLite cache row for `item` (`staleItem := cloneArtifact(originalItem); staleItem.Title = "stale-index-title"; require.NoError(t, bldb.UpsertItem(ctx, ws.DB, staleItem))`), closes `ws`, and wraps `persistArtifactWriteFn` so writes for `shipment.ID` delegate to `origWriteFn` while any write for `item.ID` increments `itemWriteCalls` and returns `fmt.Errorf("injected task restore write denial: %w", blerrors.ErrWriteNotApplied)`. Asserts `reopened, err := NewWorkspace(ctx, root)` succeeds (`require.NoError(t, err)`), `itemWriteCalls == 0`, `loadURCanonicalArtifact(t, reopened, shipment.ID)` equals `originalShipment`, `reopened` SQLite index row for `item.ID` (`bldb.GetItem(ctx, reopened.DB, item.ID)`) has `Title == originalItem.Title`, and `returnBlockedJournalPath(root, shipment.ID, item.ID)` does not exist (`os.ErrNotExist`).
       * `admitted_changed_shipment_write_denial_fails_closed_and_retains_journal`: same torn shipment state (`items: []` on disk, `item` unchanged on disk), with `persistArtifactWriteFn` injecting `fmt.Errorf("injected shipment restore write denial: %w", blerrors.ErrWriteNotApplied)` when `artifact.ID == shipment.ID`. Asserts `NewWorkspace(ctx, root)` fails with `require.ErrorIs(t, err, blerrors.ErrWriteNotApplied)` and the legacy return-blocked journal file remains on disk.
       * `non_admissible_changed_task_fails_preimage_guard_with_zero_writer_calls_and_retains_journal`: writes the legacy `return-blocked` journal (`journal.Reason == ""`, so `itemTargets` admits only `journal.Item`), mutates the on-disk task `item` (`tornItem.Status = models.StatusBlocked; persistArtifact(ctx, ws, tornItem, false)`), closes `ws`, and wraps `persistArtifactWriteFn` to count `totalWriteCalls`. Asserts `NewWorkspace(ctx, root)` fails at `itemMatches` with `require.ErrorIs(t, err, blerrors.ErrShipmentConflict)`, `totalWriteCalls == 0` (zero restore writer calls), and the legacy return-blocked journal file remains on disk.
    2. Confirm compilation (`go test -run=^$ -count=1 ./...` exits `0`) and confirm `Red Phase: CONFIRMED` against unpatched `internal/core/shipment.go` (Subtest 1 fails with `ErrWriteNotApplied` because `recoverLegacyReturnBlockedJournal` unconditionally calls `persistArtifact` on the unchanged task).
  * **Second (GREEN phase, implemented by `build-feature` in `internal/core/shipment.go`)**:
    1. In `recoverLegacyReturnBlockedJournal` (`internal/core/shipment.go:2963-3025`), while `recoverPendingShipmentOperations` (`shipment.go:2727`) holds `lockShipmentLifecycleGlobalRaw` (`2761`), `lockShipmentMembership` (`2834`), and `lockArtifactMutations` on `{journal.Shipment.ID, journal.Item.ID}` (`2840-2844`) and after `shipmentMatches` and `itemMatches` preimage validation (`lines 2979-3004`) and `persistArtifact(recoveryCtx, ws, journal.Shipment, false)` (`lines 3008-3010`):
       * Evaluate `itemPreimageMatches, err := recoveryArtifactMatchesAny(currentItem, []recoveryArtifactCandidate{{artifact: journal.Item}})` (returning wrapped error if `err != nil`) and `currentItemPath, targetItemPath, pathErr := resolveArtifactPersistPaths(recoveryCtx, ws, journal.Item, true)` (returning `fmt.Errorf("restore item %s from legacy journal: %w", journal.Item.ID, pathErr)` if `pathErr != nil`).
       * When `itemPreimageMatches && currentItemPath == targetItemPath`: skip the physical Markdown replacement (`writeArtifactFileGoverned` / `MoveFileExW`) for `journal.Item`, clone `indexedItem := cloneArtifact(currentItem)` (preserving `Level` and `HierarchyPath` from on-disk truth), call `if err := mergeDBOnlyLinksIntoArtifact(recoveryCtx, ws, indexedItem); err != nil { return fmt.Errorf("restore item %s from legacy journal: preserve database-only links: %w", journal.Item.ID, err) }`, and call `if err := bldb.UpsertItem(recoveryCtx, ws.DB, indexedItem); err != nil { return fmt.Errorf("restore item %s from legacy journal: upsert item: %w", journal.Item.ID, err) }`.
       * Otherwise (when `!itemPreimageMatches || currentItemPath != targetItemPath`, e.g. `journal.Reason != ""` where `currentItem` matched `itemTarget` with `Status == Blocked`, or requires relocation): call `if err := persistArtifact(recoveryCtx, ws, journal.Item, true); err != nil { return fmt.Errorf("restore item %s from legacy journal: %w", journal.Item.ID, err) }`.
       * Call `removeReturnBlockedJournal(recoveryCtx, ws, journal.Shipment.ID, journal.Item.ID)` only after both shipment restoration and item restoration/reconciliation succeed.

  * **Second (GREEN phase, implemented by `build-feature` in `internal/mcp/session_mux.go` and `internal/mcp/server.go`)**: Replace the `U3a` stub bodies in `internal/mcp/session_mux.go` and add unexported per-instance session-registry/mutex/drain fields on `struct Server` (with optional initialization in `newServer`) in `internal/mcp/server.go` so multiple `*Server` instances in tests remain isolated without package-level globals:
    1. `MultiSessionConn` (implementing `mcpserver.ClientSession` with unique `sessionID`, `initialized atomic.Bool`, buffered `notifications chan mcplib.JSONRPCNotification` field returned as send-only `NotificationChannel() chan<- mcplib.JSONRPCNotification` to satisfy `mcpserver.ClientSession`, mutex-guarded JSON-RPC line writer, and per-session `inFlight map[string]context.CancelFunc` keyed via a typed canonical request-ID helper `canonicalRequestIDKey(any) string` using explicit type switches / `json.Number` / `string` / `int64` rather than `fmt.Sprintf("%v", id)`).
    2. `(s *Server) ServeSessionStream(ctx context.Context, sessionID string, r io.Reader, w io.Writer) error`: registers `MultiSessionConn` via `s.mcp.RegisterSession(ctx, sess)`, runs the per-session notification writer goroutine draining `<-sess.notifications` (the struct's internal bidirectional channel backing `NotificationChannel() chan<- mcplib.JSONRPCNotification`), reads newline-delimited JSON-RPC frames from `r` via `bufio.Reader` (`ReadBytes('\n')` with a `4 MiB` max frame guard, avoiding `bufio.Scanner`'s 64 KiB `ErrTooLong` limit), intercepts `notifications/cancelled` (`params.requestId`) to cancel only that session's matching in-flight request context, dispatches JSON-RPC requests concurrently within the session using `s.mcp.WithContext(reqCtx, sess)` -> `s.mcp.HandleMessage` tracked by a per-session `sync.WaitGroup`, and on stream EOF or context cancel unregisters only `sessionID` (`s.mcp.UnregisterSession`), cancels that session's remaining in-flight contexts, waits on the session `sync.WaitGroup` for all in-flight request goroutines and the notification writer goroutine to drain before returning (ensuring zero writes to `w` after return), and updates the per-instance session registry on `s`.
    3. `(s *Server) ActiveSessionCount() int` and `(s *Server) CloseAllSessions() error` backed by unexported per-instance session-registry/mutex/drain fields on `struct Server` in `internal/mcp/server.go` (and optional initialization in `newServer`) to support `backlogit mcp status`, graceful daemon drain on `stop` / `--replace` (with `(*Server).CloseAllSessions()` extended in Wave 8 `U5` / `211.005-T` under `089-DL` / `0D073C21` to also call `if s.coordinator != nil { result = errors.Join(result, s.coordinator.Close()) }` after `<-sess.done` and before publishing `s.sessionDrainErr` and closing `s.sessionDrainDone`, which is idempotent via `c.closeOnce.Do(...)` and keeps all `TestU3_*` in `internal/mcp/session_mux_test.go` green), and per-instance `*Server` isolation in tests without package-level globals.
* **Affected files (`3` files)**:

#### U4: Implement Cross-Platform Local IPC Listener and Dialer (Windows Named Pipe Current-User DACL and Workspace-Contained POSIX `0600` UDS) (`code`) — `211.004-T`

* **Domain**: `code`.
* **Depends on**: `U3` (`211.003-T`), `U4a` (`211.018-T`) (Wave 7 of `212-S`).
* **Changes**:
  * **First (Genuine RED phase, scaffolded by `harness-architect` in `internal/cli/mcp_ipc_test.go` ONLY with zero production stub edits)**: Because `DeriveMCPDaemonEndpoint`, `ListenMCPDaemonIPC`, `DialMCPDaemonIPC`, and `isVerifiedProcessAlive` already landed on `HEAD` in Wave 5 (`U4a` `211.018-T`), `harness-architect` scaffolds **only** the behavioral test file `internal/cli/mcp_ipc_test.go` (`TestU4_*`) with zero edits to `internal/cli/mcp_ipc_windows.go` or `internal/cli/mcp_ipc_unix.go`.
  * **Second (GREEN phase, implemented by `build-feature` in `internal/cli/mcp_ipc_windows.go` and `internal/cli/mcp_ipc_unix.go`)**: Replace the `U4a` stub bodies in `internal/cli/mcp_ipc_windows.go` (`//go:build windows`) and `internal/cli/mcp_ipc_unix.go` (`//go:build !windows`) with the full behavioral implementation:
    1. `DeriveMCPDaemonEndpoint(workspaceRoot, storageRoot, runtimeDir string) (transport, endpoint string, err error)`:
       * Computes `hexHash16` from `sha256` of the canonical `storageRoot` path (lowercased on Windows).
       * On Windows: returns `("named_pipe", `\\.\pipe\backlogit-mcp-<hexHash16>`, nil)`.
       * On POSIX (`R16`, `D2`, Constitution Principle IV — strictly workspace-contained, zero `/tmp` / `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR` / `$HOME` fallback): returns `("unix", filepath.Join(runtimeDir, "mcp.sock"), nil)` when `len(filepath.Join(runtimeDir, "mcp.sock")) <= 100`. When `len(filepath.Join(runtimeDir, "mcp.sock")) > 100` and `len(filepath.Join(runtimeDir, "s")) <= 100`, falls back to the short workspace-contained socket leaf `("unix", filepath.Join(runtimeDir, "s"), nil)` inside `<storage_root>/runtime/`. When even `len(filepath.Join(runtimeDir, "s")) > 100`, returns `("", "", mcpDaemonValidationError("unix domain socket path %q (%d bytes) exceeds 100-byte sun_path limit; move the workspace to a shorter path or set BACKLOGIT_WORKSPACE_DIR to a shorter workspace-contained storage path", filepath.Join(runtimeDir, "s"), len(filepath.Join(runtimeDir, "s"))))` with zero outside-workspace fallback.
    2. `ListenMCPDaemonIPC(ctx context.Context, runtimeRoot *os.Root, runtimeDir, transport, endpoint string) (net.Listener, error)`:
       * On Windows: creates a multi-instance Windows Named Pipe listener via `golang.org/x/sys/windows` `CreateNamedPipe` (`PIPE_ACCESS_DUPLEX`, `PIPE_TYPE_BYTE | PIPE_READMODE_BYTE | PIPE_WAIT`, `PIPE_UNLIMITED_INSTANCES`, `PIPE_REJECT_REMOTE_CLIENTS`) with a current-user SID DACL (`TOKEN_USER` SDDL `D:P(A;;GA;;;<UserSID>)`), using either explicit `windows.Overlapped` event handles on `ConnectNamedPipe`/`ReadFile`/`WriteFile` or synchronous per-instance pipe handles woken on `Listener.Close()` via a loopback dial so `os.NewFile` reads/writes never fail with `ERROR_INVALID_PARAMETER`.
       * On POSIX (`docs/compound/2026-10-27-pin-the-handle-not-the-path.md`, `085-DL`, & `089-DL`): verifies `runtimeRoot != nil`, `filepath.IsAbs(runtimeDir)`, `filepath.IsAbs(endpoint)`, `len(endpoint) <= 100`, `filepath.Clean(endpoint) == endpoint` and `filepath.Dir(endpoint) == filepath.Clean(runtimeDir)` before bind (rejecting unclean `x/..` segments before `net.Listen`, and comparing against the upfront-verified `runtimeDir` rather than `runtimeRoot.Name()`, which is relative `"runtime"` when opened via `storageHandle.OpenRoot("runtime")`), and that `leafName := filepath.Base(endpoint)` is in the closed set `{"mcp.sock", "s"}` (failing closed with wrapped `corerrors.ErrValidation` if any check fails); removes any stale socket leaf via handle-relative `runtimeRoot.Remove(leafName)`; binds `net.Listen("unix", endpoint)` under a mutex-serialized `syscall.Umask(0o177)` (restored via `defer syscall.Umask(oldMask)`) so the socket inode is created atomically with `0600` owner-only permissions without any post-bind path-based `os.Chmod`; and verifies the bound socket via handle-relative `pinnedInfo, err := runtimeRoot.Lstat(leafName)` (`*os.Root`, asserting `pinnedInfo.Mode()&os.ModeSocket != 0`, `pinnedInfo.Mode().Perm() == 0o600`, and `rejectLink(pinnedInfo, endpoint)` — where on POSIX `isSymlinkOrReparse` inspects only `pinnedInfo.Mode()&os.ModeSymlink != 0` and ignores the path string, performing zero path-based syscalls, whereas on Windows IPC uses `\\.\pipe\...` kernel Named Pipes and filesystem lock/sidecar checks in `U1b` use `windows.GetFileAttributes(absLeafPath)` as the required reparse-attribute supplement because Go 1.24 reports NTFS junctions as `ModeIrregular`), using identity-checked `runtimeRoot.Remove(leafName)` cleanup if post-bind verification fails.
    3. `DialMCPDaemonIPC(ctx context.Context, transport, endpoint string, timeout time.Duration) (net.Conn, error)`: connects a client shim or status/stop probe to the local IPC endpoint within `timeout` (retrying `windows.ERROR_PIPE_BUSY` up to `timeout` on Windows). On Windows (`mcp_ipc_windows.go`), `(*mcpPipeConn).Read` returns `0, io.EOF` directly when `errors.Is(err, io.EOF)` so callers receive unwrapped `io.EOF` on peer disconnect, and `(*mcpPipeConn).await` returns `operationErr` directly when `operationErr != nil` and `cancelErr == nil && drainErr == nil` (unwrapping single-error `os.ErrDeadlineExceeded` in `Read`/`Write` or preserving `net.Error` `Timeout() == true` compliance).
    4. `isVerifiedProcessAlive(pid int, startTime int64, exePath string) bool`: performs a non-destructive read-only OS process identity check without opening or locking `mcp-daemon.lock` (`R3`, `D5`, `089-DL`), following the `unsafe`-free (Constitution Principle I) cross-platform OS process-identity contract established in `205-S` (`internal/cli/mcp_daemon_terminate_linux.go` and `internal/cli/mcp_daemon_terminate_other.go`):
       * On Windows (`internal/cli/mcp_ipc_windows.go`): `isVerifiedProcessAlive` uses `windows.OpenProcess`, `windows.GetProcessTimes` (`CreationTime.Nanoseconds() == startTime`), `windows.QueryFullProcessImageName`, and zero-timeout `windows.WaitForSingleObject(handle, 0) == windows.WAIT_TIMEOUT`.
       * On POSIX (`internal/cli/mcp_ipc_unix.go`): `isVerifiedProcessAlive` reads `/proc/<pid>/stat` field 22 directly (requiring `starttime > 0` and never using `processStartTime`'s `1` fallback when `/proc` is absent) and `/proc/<pid>/exe` (stripping any `" (deleted)"` suffix as in `mcp_daemon_terminate_linux.go` and comparing canonical `filepath.Clean` paths before re-verifying `/proc/<pid>/stat` field 22 == `start`), and **intentionally fails closed (`return false`) on non-procfs POSIX hosts** (`!windows && !linux`), matching `mcp_daemon_terminate_other.go` without modifying `internal/cli/mcp_daemon_lock_unix.go`. (In `U5`, live daemon status/attachment uses the authenticated IPC `"status"` control probe over the workspace-contained socket when `info.Endpoint != ""`.)
* **Affected files (`3` files)**:
  * `internal/cli/mcp_ipc_windows.go` (declarations already landed in `U4a` `211.018-T`)
  * `internal/cli/mcp_ipc_unix.go` (declarations already landed in `U4a` `211.018-T`)
  * `internal/cli/mcp_ipc_test.go` (new behavioral test file scaffolded by `harness-architect` with zero production stub edits; in PR #645 CI Cycle 1 commit `62064e89`, updated to open relative parent roots on Linux so `os.Root.Name()` exercises the relative-parent boundary assertion, verified in Ordinal 19 PASS `79825a5724e5cf9202650e0f91a0870420776349cc8e3deb811a0a01d1ad0222` and Ordinal 20 PASS `5be6ffa55ab7d9c39c9c3261332ccf074b56fae4d27fe19bff8bcdd2405af6e0`)
* **Acceptance criteria**:
  * **Genuine RED phase (`harness-architect`)**: `internal/cli/mcp_ipc_test.go` (`TestU4_*`) is scaffolded with zero edits to `internal/cli/mcp_ipc_windows.go` or `internal/cli/mcp_ipc_unix.go`, compiles cleanly (`go test -run=^$ -count=1 ./...` exits `0`) against the `U4a` declarations on `HEAD`, and fails deterministically on assertions (`Red Phase: CONFIRMED`).
  * Scenario 1 (`TestU4_DeriveMCPDaemonEndpointDeterministicAndWorkspaceIsolated`): verifies deterministic per-workspace endpoint derivation, distinct endpoints for distinct workspaces (`SC7a`), workspace-contained POSIX `sun_path` handling (`<storage_root>/runtime/mcp.sock` when `<=100` bytes, workspace-contained short leaf `<storage_root>/runtime/s` when `len(mcp.sock) > 100 && len(s) <= 100`, and deterministic `corerrors.ErrValidation` refusal with actionable guidance and zero `/tmp`/`$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR`/`$HOME` writes when `len(s) > 100`, `R16`, `D2`).
  * Scenario 2 (`TestU4_ListenAndDialMCPDaemonIPCMultiClient`): opens `ListenMCPDaemonIPC`, connects 4 concurrent clients via `DialMCPDaemonIPC`, exchanges framed JSON lines concurrently, and closes cleanly; also verifies `ListenMCPDaemonIPC` deterministically refuses relative endpoints, non-canonical endpoints (`filepath.Clean(endpoint) != endpoint`, e.g., `x/..` segments), foreign-directory endpoints (`filepath.Dir(endpoint) != filepath.Clean(runtimeDir)`), or disallowed leaf names with `corerrors.ErrValidation` (`085-DL`, `089-DL`).
  * Scenario 3 (`TestU4_IsVerifiedProcessAliveChecksIdentity`): verifies `isVerifiedProcessAlive` returns `true` for the current process with matching `ProcessStartTime` and `ExecutablePath` on supported procfs/Windows hosts, and `false` on mismatched `ProcessStartTime`, mismatched `ExecutablePath`, dead PID, or non-procfs POSIX hosts (`D5`, `089-DL`).
  * `go test -timeout=30m -count=1 -race -run '^TestU4_' ./internal/cli` passes (while `TestU4a_` stays green).
* **Execution posture**: `test-first`.

#### U5: Implement Background Daemon Host (`RunMCPDaemon`), Pre-Attach Identity Handshake (`VerifyMCPDaemonAttachment`), Non-Exclusive `InspectMCPDaemonStatus`, and Graceful-Drain `StopMCPDaemon` (`code`) — `211.005-T`

* **Domain**: `code`.
* **Depends on**: `U4` (`211.004-T`), `U1b` (`211.008-T`), `U5a` (`211.019-T`) (Wave 8 of `212-S`).
* **Changes**:
  * **First (Genuine RED phase, scaffolded by `harness-architect` in `internal/cli/mcp_daemon_multisession_test.go` ONLY with zero production stub edits)**: Because `MCPDaemonInfo` / `MCPDaemonStatus` fields (`IPCTransport`, `Endpoint`, `ActiveSessions`, `ProtocolVersion`) and `VerifyMCPDaemonAttachment` / `RunMCPDaemon` declarations already landed on `HEAD` in Wave 5 (`U5a` `211.019-T`), `harness-architect` scaffolds **only** the behavioral test file `internal/cli/mcp_daemon_multisession_test.go` (`TestU5_*`, landed at `15ac1682d0a658039f1ac87c065bfc5eb1aa0bd4`) with zero edits to `internal/cli/mcp_daemon.go` or `internal/mcp/session_mux.go`.
  * **Second (GREEN phase, implemented by `build-feature` in `internal/mcp/session_mux.go` and `internal/cli/mcp_daemon.go` without touching `internal/cli/root.go`)**:
    0. **Zero-new-symbol coordinator drain in `(*Server).CloseAllSessions()` (`internal/mcp/session_mux.go`, adjudicating `0D073C21` under `089-DL` / Option 5)**: Because `s.coordinator` is unexported in `package mcp` while `RunMCPDaemon` lives in `package cli` (`internal/cli/mcp_daemon.go`), and because `(*OperationCoordinator).Close()` (`internal/mcp/coordinator.go:288-292`) is idempotent (`c.closeOnce.Do(func() { close(c.closed) }); <-c.loopDone`) and no test in `internal/mcp/session_mux_test.go` calls `InvokeTool` after `CloseAllSessions()`, update `(*Server).CloseAllSessions()` in `internal/mcp/session_mux.go` (lines 543–574) so that after waiting for all sessions to drain (`<-sess.done`), it calls `if s.coordinator != nil { result = errors.Join(result, s.coordinator.Close()) }` before publishing `s.sessionDrainErr` and closing `s.sessionDrainDone` (and update its GoDoc comment accordingly). This stops the background `c.schedule()` goroutine and drains both active sessions and the `OperationCoordinator` via a single `s.CloseAllSessions()` call without adding any new exported method to `*mcp.Server`.
    1. Keep the `U5a` `MCPDaemonInfo` and `MCPDaemonStatus` fields (`IPCTransport string` `json:"ipc_transport,omitempty"`, `Endpoint string` `json:"endpoint,omitempty"`, `ActiveSessions int` `json:"active_sessions"`, and `ProtocolVersion string` `json:"protocol_version,omitempty"`) and `PrewarmMCPDaemon` / `RunMCPSessionShim` stubs intact.
    2. Update `InspectMCPDaemonStatus` (and `StopMCPDaemon`'s initial not-running check) to perform **non-exclusive probing first** (`R3`, `D5`, `A045DBA4`): first perform a lock-free `runtimeRoot.Lstat(mcpDaemonLockName)` + `rejectLink(info, filepath.Join(runtimeDir, mcpDaemonLockName))` check using the verified absolute `runtimeDir` propagated by `U1b` (`211.008-T`) (preserving `TestU2_RejectsSymlinkOrReparseRuntimeDir/lock_leaf_symlink` and `TestU1b_NestedRuntimeRootUsesCanonicalAbsolutePathForReparseValidation`), then read `"mcp-daemon.json"` via `readMCPDaemonInfoAt(runtimeRoot, runtimeDir)` without locking `mcp-daemon.lock`. If `"mcp-daemon.json"` is absent (`os.ErrNotExist`), return `Running: false` immediately without locking `mcp-daemon.lock`. If present and `isVerifiedProcessAlive` is `true`, query live `ActiveSessions` over a `500ms` IPC `"status"` control frame when `info.Endpoint != ""` (tolerating empty `info.Endpoint` from lock-only test holders in `TestU3_`/`TestU4_`) and return `Running: true` without touching `mcp-daemon.lock`. Only when `"mcp-daemon.json"` exists on disk and `isVerifiedProcessAlive` is `false` (crashed holder), acquire `openMCPDaemonLockFile` to remove the stale sidecar under lock. In `StopMCPDaemon` (and `--replace`), when a verified daemon with a non-empty `Endpoint` is running, send a bounded (`500ms`) IPC `"stop"` control frame to trigger graceful `s.CloseAllSessions()` drain (which drains all active sessions and closes `s.coordinator` in `internal/mcp/session_mux.go`, `089-DL` / `0D073C21`) before falling back to `TerminateVerifiedProcess` (`R11`).
    3. Replace the `U5a` stub bodies of `VerifyMCPDaemonAttachment(info MCPDaemonInfo, expectedWorkspaceRoot, expectedStorageRoot, expectedExePath, expectedVersion string) error` and `RunMCPDaemon(ctx context.Context, rootPath string, replace bool, idleTimeout time.Duration) error`:
       * `VerifyMCPDaemonAttachment` checks canonical `WorkspaceRoot`, `StorageRoot`, `ExecutablePath`, and `Version` before attaching, returning `corerrors.ErrDaemonWorkspaceMismatch` (`retryable: false`) on workspace/storage mismatch or `corerrors.ErrDaemonVersionMismatch` (with daemon PID, running vs expected version/binary path, `active_sessions`, and `backlogit mcp stop` / `backlogit mcp --replace` remediation) on binary/version skew without killing active peer sessions (`R15`, `SC7a`, `SC7b`, `D4`).
       * `RunMCPDaemon` acquires `mcp-daemon.lock`, opens `ListenMCPDaemonIPC`, writes `mcp-daemon.json` with `IPCTransport`, `Endpoint`, and `ProtocolVersion: "backlogit-mcp-ipc/v1"`, and accepts connections. On each accepted `net.Conn`, it reads the initial `backlogit-mcp-ipc/v1` control header line via a `*bufio.Reader`, answers `"status"` and `"stop"` control probes directly without calling `s.ServeSessionStream` or incrementing `ActiveSessionCount()`, and for verified `"session"` connections passes that same `*bufio.Reader` (`r`) and `conn` (`w`) into `s.ServeSessionStream(ctx, sessionID, reader, conn)` so pipelined `initialize` bytes buffered after the handshake newline are never lost. On graceful `"stop"` IPC control frame, deterministic idle auto-shutdown (`DefaultDaemonIdleTimeout = 15 * time.Minute` when `active_sessions == 0`, `R11`, `D3`), or context cancellation, `RunMCPDaemon` closes the IPC listener, calls `s.CloseAllSessions()` (draining all active sessions and closing `s.coordinator`), closes `s.Workspace` when non-nil, removes `mcp-daemon.json`, and releases `mcp-daemon.lock`.
* **Affected files (`3` files)**:

#### U1i: Implement Bounded Exponential Backoff Retry for Transient Windows `ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED` and `ERROR_SHARING_VIOLATION` in `atomicfile.atomicReplace` (`code`) — `211.020-T`

* **Domain**: `code` (executed under **`careful` + `freeze-scope`** safety modes per Constitution Principle VIII, bounding edits strictly to `internal/atomicfile/atomicfile_windows.go` and `internal/atomicfile/atomicfile_windows_test.go`; modifies existing unexported `atomicReplace` and adds unexported package-level sleep seam `atomicReplaceSleep` and predicate `isTransientWindowsReplaceError` with **zero new exported symbols**).
