* **Changes**:
  * **First (Genuine RED phase, scaffolded by `harness-architect` in `internal/atomicfile/atomicfile_windows_test.go` with zero production files modified)**:
    1. In `internal/atomicfile/atomicfile_windows_test.go` (sequential — **no `t.Parallel()`** on any test that swaps `moveFileEx` or `atomicReplaceSleep`, per `docs/compound/2026-07-29-durable-writes-test-seam-patterns.md`): add `TestU1i_AtomicReplaceRetriesTransientWindowsErrorsWithExponentialBackoff(t *testing.T)` driving `atomicReplace` and `WriteFileAtomic` through the existing `moveFileEx` seam (and `atomicReplaceSleep`) so that against unpatched `atomicfile_windows.go`, Subcase 1 fails deterministically on assertions because `atomicReplace` fails on attempt `1` without retrying transient `windows.ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED` or `windows.ERROR_SHARING_VIOLATION`.
       * **Compile-Clean RED-Phase Seam Rule (`P-002.1`)**: Because `harness-architect` modifies **zero** production files during RED scaffolding, `TestU1i_*` in `internal/atomicfile/atomicfile_windows_test.go` MUST compile cleanly (`go test -run=^$ -count=1 ./...` exits `0`) against unmodified `internal/atomicfile/atomicfile_windows.go` (which currently declares `moveFileEx` and `atomicReplace` at lines 31–56, and does not yet declare `atomicReplaceSleep`). To ensure `TestU1i_*` compiles cleanly in RED with zero production edits AND runs deterministically in microseconds once `build-feature` lands `atomicReplaceSleep = time.Sleep` (or `atomicReplace` retry): `TestU1i_*` swaps the **existing** `moveFileEx` seam (`orig := moveFileEx; t.Cleanup(func() { moveFileEx = orig })`) to count attempts and inject `windows.ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED` and `windows.ERROR_SHARING_VIOLATION` (plus `errors.New("simulated MoveFileEx failure")` and `windows.ERROR_FILE_NOT_FOUND`), while `internal/atomicfile/atomicfile_windows.go` in GREEN declares `var atomicReplaceSleep = time.Sleep` (`6` retries / `7` total attempts with initial delay `1ms` doubling each retry: `1ms, 2ms, 4ms, 8ms, 16ms, 32ms` = `63ms` max total wait, plus immediate attempt-1 fast-fail with `0` retries and `0` sleeps when the destination/target path carries `windows.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_READONLY` or `windows.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY`).
       * **Subcase 1 (`transient_access_denied_and_sharing_violation_succeed_within_7_attempts`)**: Swaps `moveFileEx` to return `windows.ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED` on attempts 1–2, `windows.ERROR_SHARING_VIOLATION` on attempt 3, and delegate to `orig` on attempt 4 while calling `WriteFileAtomic(path, []byte("replacement"))` over an existing file containing `"original"`. Asserts `require.NoError(t, err)`, `attempts == 4`, and destination content equals `"replacement"` (fails deterministically in RED against unpatched `atomicfile_windows.go` with `attempts == 1` and `ErrWriteNotApplied`). Also verifies success on the boundary `7`th attempt (`6` transient failures followed by `nil` on attempt `7`).
       * **Subcase 2 (`exhausted_transient_retries_return_write_not_applied_after_7_attempts_and_leave_original_intact`)**: Swaps `moveFileEx` to always return `windows.ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED` (and in a second check `windows.ERROR_SHARING_VIOLATION`) during `WriteFileAtomic(path, []byte("replacement"))` over an existing file containing `"original"`. Asserts `require.Error(t, err)`, `assert.True(t, blerrors.IsWriteNotApplied(err))`, `assert.ErrorIs(t, err, windows.ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED)`, `assert.Equal(t, 7, attempts, "must attempt 1 initial + 6 retries = 7 total attempts")`, and destination file content remains `"original"`.
       * **Subcase 3 (`non_transient_and_readonly_or_directory_target_errors_fail_immediately_on_attempt_1_with_zero_retries`)**: Swaps `moveFileEx` to return `errors.New("non-transient failure")` (and `windows.ERROR_FILE_NOT_FOUND`), and tests destination paths carrying `windows.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_READONLY` (`0o400`) or `windows.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY`. Asserts `atomicReplace` fails immediately on attempt `1` with `attempts == 1` (`0` retries), `blerrors.IsWriteNotApplied(err) == true`, and `0` sleeps.
  * **Second (GREEN phase, implemented by `build-feature` in `internal/atomicfile/atomicfile_windows.go`)**:
    1. In `internal/atomicfile/atomicfile_windows.go`:
       * Define constants `atomicReplaceMaxRetries = 6` and `atomicReplaceInitialBackoff = 1 * time.Millisecond`, and unexported sleep seam `var atomicReplaceSleep = time.Sleep`.
       * Define unexported helper `isTransientWindowsReplaceError(err error) bool` returning `errors.Is(err, windows.ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED) || errors.Is(err, windows.ERROR_SHARING_VIOLATION)`, and skip backoff retry immediately (fail on attempt `1` with `0` retries and `0` sleeps) when the destination/target path carries `windows.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_READONLY` or `windows.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY` (so negative tests in `internal/core` that intentionally make a target file read-only `0o400` or a directory fail in `0ms` instead of sleeping).
       * Update `atomicReplace(tmpName, path string, durable bool) error` to compute `flags := moveFileExFlags(durable)` and loop for `attempt := 0; ; attempt++`: call `err := moveFileEx(tmpName, path, flags)`; if `err == nil`, return `nil`; if `!isTransientWindowsReplaceError(err) || hasPermanentReplaceDenialAttribute(path) || attempt >= atomicReplaceMaxRetries`, return `fmt.Errorf("move temp over destination: %w", fmt.Errorf("%w: %w", blerrors.ErrWriteNotApplied, err))`; otherwise call `atomicReplaceSleep(atomicReplaceInitialBackoff << attempt)` (`1ms, 2ms, 4ms, 8ms, 16ms, 32ms` = `63ms` max total wait) and retry.
* **Affected files (`2` files)**:
  * `internal/atomicfile/atomicfile_windows.go`
  * `internal/atomicfile/atomicfile_windows_test.go`

  * All subcases of `TestU1i_AtomicReplaceRetriesTransientWindowsErrorsWithExponentialBackoff` (including `6` retries / `7` total attempts `1ms..32ms` = `63ms` max total wait and `0`-retry fast-fail on `FILE_ATTRIBUTE_READONLY` / `FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY` target paths) and all existing `TestMoveFileExFlags_*`, `TestAtomicReplace_*`, and `TestWriteFileAtomic_*` tests pass with `0` failures and `0` skips.
  * Scoped verification command passes: `go test -timeout=30m -count=1 -race -run '^(TestU1i_|TestAtomicReplace_|TestWriteFileAtomic_|TestMoveFileExFlags_)' ./internal/atomicfile`.

  * **First (Genuine RED phase, scaffolded by `harness-architect` in `internal/core/shipment_ops_security_windows_test.go` with zero production files modified)**:
    1. **Compile-Clean RED-Phase Seam Rule (`P-002.1`)**: Because `harness-architect` modifies **zero** production files during RED scaffolding, `TestU1j_*` in `internal/core/shipment_ops_security_windows_test.go` MUST compile cleanly (`go test -run=^$ -count=1 ./...` exits `0`) against unmodified `internal/core/shipment_ops_windows.go` AND fail deterministically on assertions (`Red Phase: CONFIRMED`) before `build-feature` lands the retry logic and unexported seams in `internal/core/shipment_ops_windows.go`. How do we induce a deterministic, compile-clean transient Windows `STATUS_SHARING_VIOLATION` / `STATUS_ACCESS_DENIED` on `renameShipmentOpsWindowsChild`, `removeShipmentOperationJournalTempFile`, and `openShipmentOpsWindowsChild` in `TestU1j_*` without referencing not-yet-declared production seams?
       * On Windows (`//go:build windows`), holding an open handle to the target journal file or temp file via `windows.CreateFile` (or `os.OpenFile`) with `shareMode = 0` (exclusive — no `FILE_SHARE_DELETE` / `FILE_SHARE_READ` / `FILE_SHARE_WRITE`) or `FILE_SHARE_READ` (without `FILE_SHARE_DELETE`) causes `openShipmentOpsWindowsChild` (`NtCreateFile`), `renameShipmentOpsWindowsChild` (`NtSetInformationFile` `FileRenameInformation` replacing the existing destination journal), and `removeShipmentOperationJournalTempFile` (`NtCreateFile` with `windows.DELETE`) to deterministically return real kernel `STATUS_SHARING_VIOLATION` / `STATUS_ACCESS_DENIED`!
       * When a background goroutine closes that contending handle after `15ms` (well within the `63ms` bounded retry window), unpatched `internal/core/shipment_ops_windows.go` (`0` retries) fails immediately on attempt `1` (`Red Phase: CONFIRMED`), whereas patched `internal/core/shipment_ops_windows.go` retries with exponential backoff (`1ms, 2ms, 4ms, 8ms, 16ms, 32ms` = `63ms` max total wait) and succeeds (`Green Phase: CONFIRMED`)!
       * Specifically, in `internal/core/shipment_ops_security_windows_test.go` (sequential — **no `t.Parallel()`**): add `TestU1j_ShipmentOpsJournalWindowsTransientContentionRetryAndSecurityPreservation(t *testing.T)` with three deterministic subcases using only existing `package core` and `os`/`golang.org/x/sys/windows` APIs:

Asserts `require.NoError(t, writeErr)` and verifies the replaced journal on disk equals `committedPayload` (fails deterministically in RED against unpatched `shipment_ops_windows.go` with `replace shipment operation journal: rename shipment operation temp file ...: The process cannot access the file because it is being used by another process.` / `Access is denied.`).
         * **Subcase 2 (`temp_remove_and_stale_temp_create_retry_transient_contention_and_fail_after_bounded_exhaustion`)**: Creates a writer-owned temp file `tempName` in `opsRoot`, holds a transient exclusive handle (`shareMode = 0`) that closes after `15ms`, and calls `removeShipmentOperationJournalTempFile(dir, opsRoot, tempName)` — asserting `require.NoError(t, removeErr)` and `os.ErrNotExist` on disk; then creates `tempName` again and holds the exclusive handle for the entire call (registered with `t.Cleanup` close) while calling `removeShipmentOperationJournalTempFile(dir, opsRoot, tempName)` — asserting `require.Error(t, err)` and `errors.Is(err, windows.ERROR_SHARING_VIOLATION)` after exhausting the bounded `7` attempts (`1` initial + `6` retries = `~63ms`).
         * **Subcase 3 (`validation_reparse_and_readonly_or_directory_target_errors_never_retry`)**: Calls `renameShipmentOpsWindowsChild(dir, "non-writer-owned.tmp", testShipmentOperationJournalName)` and `removeShipmentOperationJournalTempFile(dir, opsRoot, "../escape")` (and tests a reparse/directory/read-only `0o400` child target), asserting immediate failure on attempt `1` with `0` retries and `0` sleeps.
  * **Second (GREEN phase, implemented by `build-feature` in `internal/core/shipment_ops_windows.go`)**:
    1. In `internal/core/shipment_ops_windows.go`:
       * Define constants `shipmentOpsRetryMaxRetries = 6` and `shipmentOpsRetryInitialBackoff = 1 * time.Millisecond`, and unexported package-level seams `var shipmentOpsNtRenameFn = windows.NtSetInformationFile`, `var shipmentOpsSetDispositionFn = windows.SetFileInformationByHandle`, and `var shipmentOpsRetrySleep = time.Sleep`.
       * Define unexported helper `isTransientShipmentOpsWindowsError(err error) bool`: returns `false` if `err == nil || errors.Is(err, blerrors.ErrValidation)`; if `var status windows.NTStatus; errors.As(err, &status)` is true, returns `status == windows.STATUS_ACCESS_DENIED || status == windows.STATUS_SHARING_VIOLATION || status == windows.STATUS_DELETE_PENDING` (excluding `STATUS_FILE_IS_A_DIRECTORY` so directory/reparse refusals are never misclassified via `status.Errno()`); otherwise normalizes `norm := shipmentOpsWindowsError(err)` and returns `errors.Is(norm, windows.ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED) || errors.Is(norm, windows.ERROR_SHARING_VIOLATION) || errors.Is(norm, windows.STATUS_DELETE_PENDING)`.
       * Skip backoff retry immediately (fail on attempt `1` with `0` retries and `0` sleeps) when the destination/target path carries `windows.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_READONLY` or `windows.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY` (so negative tests in `internal/core` that intentionally make a target file read-only `0o400` or a directory fail in `0ms` instead of sleeping).
       * In `openShipmentOpsWindowsChild(dir *os.File, name string, access uint32, disposition uint32) (windows.Handle, error)`: after validating `dir != nil` and `validateShipmentOpsWindowsChildName(name)`, when `disposition == windows.FILE_OPEN` and the target does not carry `FILE_ATTRIBUTE_READONLY` or `FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY` (or when opening for write/delete is not blocked by permanent `FILE_ATTRIBUTE_READONLY` / `FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY`), retry the `windows.NtCreateFile` call up to `6` times (`7` total attempts) with `shipmentOpsRetrySleep(1 * time.Millisecond << attempt)` (`1ms, 2ms, 4ms, 8ms, 16ms, 32ms` = `63ms` max total wait) when `isTransientShipmentOpsWindowsError(err)` is true.
       * In `renameShipmentOpsWindowsChild(dir *os.File, oldName, newName string) error`: preserve the upfront `shipmentOperationJournalTempTarget`, `openShipmentOpsWindowsChild`, `validateShipmentOpsWindowsFileHandle(handle, oldName)` (`blerrors.ErrValidation` never retried), and `shipmentOpsWindowsRenameInfo` checks; skip retry immediately (`0` retries, `0` sleeps) if target `newName` carries `FILE_ATTRIBUTE_READONLY` or `FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY`; otherwise retry `shipmentOpsNtRenameFn(handle, &iosb, &renameInfo[0], uint32(len(renameInfo)), windows.FileRenameInformation)` up to `6` times (`7` total attempts) with `shipmentOpsRetrySleep(1 * time.Millisecond << attempt)` (`1ms..32ms` = `63ms` max total wait) when `isTransientShipmentOpsWindowsError(err)` is true, normalizing `err = shipmentOpsWindowsError(err)` on failure.
       * In `removeShipmentOperationJournalTempFile(dir *os.File, _ string, name string) error`: preserve the upfront `shipmentOperationJournalTempTarget`, `openShipmentOpsWindowsChild`, and `validateShipmentOpsWindowsFileHandle(handle, name)` (`blerrors.ErrValidation` never retried) checks; skip retry immediately (`0` retries, `0` sleeps) if `name` carries `FILE_ATTRIBUTE_READONLY` or `FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY`; otherwise retry `shipmentOpsSetDispositionFn(handle, windows.FileDispositionInfo, &deleteFile, 1)` up to `6` times (`7` total attempts) with `shipmentOpsRetrySleep(1 * time.Millisecond << attempt)` (`1ms..32ms` = `63ms` max total wait) when `isTransientShipmentOpsWindowsError(err)` is true, normalizing `err = shipmentOpsWindowsError(err)` on failure.
    2. In `internal/core/shipment_ops_security_windows_test.go` (`swapShipmentOpsDirectoryObject` at `line 655`, `089-DL` / `EE98533F` same-contract follow-up):
       * Retry `os.Rename(opsRoot, opsRoot+"-original")` using the same bounded exponential backoff (`1ms, 2ms, 4ms, 8ms, 16ms, 32ms` = `63ms` max total wait across `6` retries / `7` attempts when `isRetryableShipmentOpsError(err)` / `isTransientShipmentOpsWindowsError(err)` is true, e.g., via `retryShipmentOpsWindowsOperation(func() error { return os.Rename(opsRoot, opsRoot+"-original") }, nil)`) so a transient Windows Defender minifilter handle on the just-written `ops/001-S.json` file does not fail `TestShipmentOpsJournalWrite_BindsCreateAndRenameToValidatedDirectoryObjectAfterPathSwap` with `rename .backlogit/ops -> .backlogit/ops-original: Access is denied`.

#### U1k: Isolate `ExecGitRunner.Verify` from Ancestor Checkouts Under Workspace-Contained `TEMP` and Cache `ExecVersionRunner.Version` Probes (`code`) — `211.022-T`

* **Domain**: `code` (executed under **`careful` + `freeze-scope`** safety modes per Constitution Principle VIII, bounding edits strictly to the `20` declared Go files `internal/core/gate/baseref.go`, `internal/core/gate/probe.go`, `internal/core/gate/probe_test.go`, `internal/core/barrier_seam_ast_test.go`, `internal/core/shipment_reconcile_evidence_test.go`, `internal/core/custody_exec_test.go`, `internal/core/shipment_test.go`, `internal/core/shipment_reconstitute_test.go`, `internal/core/shipment_manifest_test.go`, and `internal/core/lintfix_175_062_test.go`..`068`, `070`..`072`, `095`; modifies existing methods/helpers with **zero new exported symbols**).
* **Depends on**: `U1i` (`211.020-T`, `done`) and `U1j` (`211.021-T`, `done`), placing `211.022-T` in Wave 12 (`status: done` preserved for the `089-DL` / `EE98533F` / Ordinal 14 / Ordinal 15 / Ordinal 16 same-contract follow-ups).
* **Changes**:
  * **First (Genuine RED phase, scaffolded by `harness-architect` in `internal/core/gate/probe_test.go` with zero production files modified)**:
    1. In `internal/core/gate/probe_test.go`: add `TestU1k_ExecGitRunnerVerifyIsolatesTempDirFromOuterCheckout` and `TestU1k_ExecVersionRunnerCachesProbesByBinaryIdentity` (sequential — **no `t.Parallel()`**) using only existing `package gate` types (`ExecGitRunner`, `ExecVersionRunner`, `HeadRef`, `ResolveBaseRef`, `BaseRefInput`) and standard library helpers so `go test -run=^$ -count=1 ./...` compiles cleanly with zero production edits and `go test -count=1 -run '^TestU1k_' ./internal/core/gate` fails deterministically on assertions (`Red Phase: CONFIRMED`) against unpatched `baseref.go` and `probe.go`:
       * **Subcase 1 (`TestU1k_ExecGitRunnerVerifyIsolatesTempDirFromOuterCheckout`)**: Creates an outer Git repository with an initial commit at `outerRepo`, sets `TMP`/`TEMP`/`TMPDIR` (`t.Setenv`) to a subdirectory `customTemp := filepath.Join(outerRepo, "temp")` (`os.Mkdir`), and creates a non-git child workspace `nonrepo := t.TempDir()`. Calls `got, err := (ExecGitRunner{Dir: nonrepo, Env: env}).Verify(ctx, "HEAD")`. Against unpatched `baseref.go`, `git rev-parse --verify --quiet HEAD^{commit}` walks up through `customTemp` (`os.TempDir()`) into `outerRepo/.git` and returns `got == true` (`Red Phase: CONFIRMED`); against patched `baseref.go`, `GIT_CEILING_DIRECTORIES=<filepath.Clean(os.TempDir())>` stops Git at `customTemp` so `Verify` returns `got == false, err == nil` (`Green Phase: CONFIRMED`). Then initializes a real Git repository with a commit inside `repo := t.TempDir()` (strictly inside `os.TempDir()`) and verifies `(ExecGitRunner{Dir: subdir, Env: env}).Verify(ctx, "HEAD")` returns `got == true, err == nil`, and verifies an explicit `GIT_CEILING_DIRECTORIES=` entry already present in `ExecGitRunner.Env` is preserved without duplication.
       * **Subcase 2 (`TestU1k_ExecVersionRunnerCachesProbesByBinaryIdentity`)**: Creates a local shell fixture in `t.TempDir()` that increments a counter file on each invocation and prints `"autoharness 1.6.0\n"`. Verifies that two `ExecVersionRunner` calls with the same `Binary` and the same `Dir` hit the cache (`counter == 1`), that two `ExecVersionRunner` calls with the same `Binary` (resolved to an absolute path where `filepath.IsAbs(path)` is `true`) and **different `Dir` (`t.TempDir()`)** also hit the cache (`counter == 1`, proving cross-workspace cache reuse when `filepath.IsAbs(path)` is `true`), and that modifying the binary size/mtime or `Env` slice invalidates the cache and increments the counter.
  * **Second (GREEN phase and `089-DL` / `EE98533F` / Ordinal 14 / Ordinal 15 / Ordinal 16 same-contract follow-ups across `internal/core/gate/baseref.go`, `internal/core/gate/probe.go`, `internal/core/gate/probe_test.go`, `internal/core/barrier_seam_ast_test.go`, `internal/core/shipment_reconcile_evidence_test.go`, `internal/core/custody_exec_test.go`, `internal/core/shipment_test.go`, `internal/core/shipment_reconstitute_test.go`, `internal/core/shipment_manifest_test.go`, and `internal/core/lintfix_175_062_test.go`..`068`, `070`..`072`, `095`)**:
    1. In `internal/core/gate/baseref.go` (`ExecGitRunner.Verify`):
       * When `g.Dir != ""`, compute `tempDir := filepath.Clean(os.TempDir())`. If `g.Dir` is strictly inside `tempDir` and no `"GIT_CEILING_DIRECTORIES="` entry is already present in `cmd.Env`, append `"GIT_CEILING_DIRECTORIES=" + tempDir` to `cmd.Env` so `git rev-parse --verify --quiet <ref>^{commit}` stops at `os.TempDir()` (`bin/_t1`) and never walks up into the outer repository checkout (`C:\Source\GitHub\backlogit\.git`) for non-git `t.TempDir()` workspaces, while still resolving normally for any test that runs `git init` inside its `t.TempDir()`.
    2. In `internal/core/gate/probe.go` (`ExecVersionRunner.Version` & `versionCacheKey`) and `internal/core/gate/probe_test.go`:
       * Cache successful version strings in an unexported package-level `var execVersionCache sync.Map` keyed by `execVersionCacheKey{path, modTime, size, dir, childEnv}`.
       * In `versionCacheKey(binary, dir string, childEnv []string)`: when `filepath.IsAbs(path)` is `true`, set `key.dir = ""` (only retaining `dir` when `!filepath.IsAbs(path)`), because `ExecVersionRunner{Binary: "autoharness", Dir: ws.RootPath}` resolves `autoharness` to an absolute `PATH` binary whose version output is independent of `ws.RootPath`; including `ws.RootPath` (`t.TempDir()`) in the key caused `execVersionCache` to miss on every test workspace (`0%` cross-workspace hit rate). Add a regression assertion in `TestU1k_ExecVersionRunnerCachesProbesByBinaryIdentity` proving that two `ExecVersionRunner` calls with the same `Binary` and different `Dir` (`t.TempDir()`) hit the cache (`counter == 1`).
    3. In `internal/core/barrier_seam_ast_test.go` (`TestU172_ItemLogBarrierSetterUnreachableFromProduction`):
       * Skip `name == "bin" || strings.HasPrefix(name, "_")` in addition to `strings.HasPrefix(name, ".") || name == "vendor" || name == "testdata" || name == "node_modules"` so `filepath.WalkDir(moduleRoot, ...)` does not walk `bin/_authoring_gomodcache` (`359,000` files) and completes within the `< 5.0s` runtime expectation (measured `1.18s`–`3.01s` under the pinned Go 1.24 toolchain, down from `227.92s` in Ordinal 11; no unestablished `< 1.0s` sub-second claim is required).
    4. In `internal/core/shipment_reconcile_evidence_test.go` and `internal/core/custody_exec_test.go`:
       * Cache the static git repository seed in `setupShipmentReconcileEvidenceFixture` (`45` `git` commands) and `custExecNewRepo` (`7` `git` commands) using a process-wide `sync.Once` template directory under `os.TempDir()` and copy the template tree into each test's `t.TempDir()` (removing the obsolete unused `initEvidenceGitRepo` helper), eliminating `2,400+` redundant `git.exe` subprocesses across `internal/core` (saving `~500s` = `8.3 minutes`).
    5. In `internal/core/shipment_test.go` (`setupShipmentWorkspace`, called `361+` times across `internal/core`), `internal/core/shipment_reconstitute_test.go` (`newReconstituteFixture`), `internal/core/shipment_manifest_test.go` (`newU1Fixture`), and `internal/core/custody_exec_test.go` (`custExecNewRepo`):

       * In `internal/core/shipment_reconstitute_test.go` (`newReconstituteFixture`) and `internal/core/shipment_manifest_test.go` (`newU1Fixture`): seed their static multi-artifact workspaces (`11` artifacts in `newReconstituteFixture`, `7` artifacts in `newU1Fixture`) once via `sync.Once` (`PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` + `Close()`) and copy into each test's `t.TempDir()` before opening the workspace and loading the seeded artifacts, shaving `~450–600 seconds` (`7.5–10 minutes`) off `internal/core` so all `46` packages complete well within the `2,700`-second (`45m0s`) budget.

#### U9: Implement Level 1 Toolchain Manifest Loader, Local Binary/Config/Version-Pinning Validator, and Honest Prerequisite/Remote/Unsupported Reporter (`code`) — `212.002-T`

* **Domain**: `code`.
* **Depends on**: `U8` (`212.001-T`).
* **Changes**:
  * In `internal/toolchain/types.go` and `internal/toolchain/verify.go`:
    1. Implement `DefaultWorkspaceToolchainManifest(rootPath string)` and `LoadToolchainManifest(rootPath string)`: reads optional `<storage_root>/toolchain.yaml` via `*os.Root` no-follow handle with duplicate-YAML-key rejection (`docs/compound/2026-08-31-duplicate-yaml-keys-survive-safe-load-validation.md`), or synthesizes the default workspace tool inventory covering `backlogit`, companion MCP servers (`engram`, `graphtor-docs`, `context7`, `tavily`, `github`), CLI helpers (`bun`, `bunx`, `.copilot/graphtor-mcp-shim.cjs`), `host-prerequisite` entries (`pwsh`, `bash`, `git`, `gh`, `go`, `copilot`), and `built-in-host` tools.
    2. Implement `VerifyWorkspaceToolchain(ctx context.Context, rootPath string, manifest *ToolchainManifest) (*ToolchainVerificationReport, error)`:
       * Sets `ContainmentLevel: "level-1-workspace-local-pinned"` and `OSContainerSandboxDeferred: true` (`R18`, `R23`).
       * Resolves the expected absolute workspace root and each expected relative tool path once up front before opening the workspace `*os.Root` pin (never re-resolving or calling `EvalSymlinks` inside the pin window per `docs/compound/2026-10-27-pin-the-handle-not-the-path.md`).
       * For `workspace-local-pinned` entries: verifies workspace-relative path confinement inside `rootPath` via the pinned workspace `*os.Root` handle (`rootHandle.Lstat(relPath)` + `!core.IsSymlinkOrReparsePoint(info, absPath)` followed by `rootHandle.Open(relPath)` and `os.SameFile(opened, linked)` per `docs/compound/2026-10-27-pin-the-handle-not-the-path.md`, rejecting bare global `PATH` names, `..` traversal, absolute external paths, cross-workspace paths, and symlinks/junctions to external files), file existence, non-empty `PinnedVersion` or lockfile provenance, optional SHA-256 match, and workspace-scoped binding (`--cwd`, `COPILOT_HOME`, `ENGRAM_DATA_DIR`, `SC8`–`SC12`):
         * A `workspace-local-pinned` `ToolEntry` must declare **at least one** of a non-empty `pinned_version` (`strings.TrimSpace(tool.PinnedVersion) != ""`) **or** a non-empty `lockfile_path` (`strings.TrimSpace(tool.LockfilePath) != ""`); if both are empty, `VerifyWorkspaceToolchain` rejects the entry (`missing version or lockfile pin`).
         * Whenever `strings.TrimSpace(tool.LockfilePath) != ""` is specified (whether alone as lockfile-only provenance or alongside `pinned_version`), `verifyLocalTool` opens `lockfile_path` via the pinned workspace `*os.Root` handle (`openLocal`), reads its UTF-8 content, and verifies: (1) the lockfile is non-empty (`len(bytes.TrimSpace(data)) > 0`), (2) the lockfile content contains the tool's trimmed `ID` (`strings.Contains(string(data), strings.TrimSpace(tool.ID))`), and (3) when `strings.TrimSpace(tool.PinnedVersion) != ""` is also set, the lockfile content also contains the trimmed `PinnedVersion` (`strings.Contains(string(data), strings.TrimSpace(tool.PinnedVersion))`). If any of (1)–(3) fails, `verifyLocalTool` returns a wrapped `blerrors.ErrValidation` (`invalid lockfile provenance`), causing `VerifyWorkspaceToolchain` to record a `violation` finding (`Status: "violation"`, `ContainmentClass: NonConformant`, `Code: "local_tool_invalid"`).
       * Emits explicit, structured `ToolVerificationFinding` records for `host-prerequisite`, `remote-declared`, `built-in-host`, `unsupported`, and `non-conformant` entries rather than silently counting them as workspace-contained (`R20`, `R21`, `SC13`).
    3. Implement `ValidateMCPConfigPinning(rootPath string, mcpJSONPath string, manifest *ToolchainManifest) ([]ToolVerificationFinding, error)`: parses `.mcp.json`, verifies 100% inventory accounting (`SC11`, flagging any uninventoried shadow server with `Code: "uninventoried_shadow_tool"`), verifies `backlogit` points to `./bin/backlogit.exe` (`R12`), and flags bare global `PATH` commands or cross-workspace paths (`SC12`, `SC13`).
    4. **Authoritative U9 Transport & Scope Contract (No Re-Harnessing Required; Ready for Step 4.4 Review Gate at `f078fc3f`)**:
       * `WorkspaceLocalPinned` MCP server entries require `server.Type == ""` or exact canonical lowercase `server.Type == "stdio"` (rejecting any other `server.Type`, including uppercase `"STDIO"`, and rejecting non-empty `server.URL`).
       * `RemoteDeclared` MCP server entries reject `strings.EqualFold(server.Type, "stdio")`, require empty `server.Command`, `server.Args`, and `server.Env`, and validate non-empty `server.URL` as `http` or `https` with a non-empty host.
       * `HostPrerequisite` and `BuiltInHost` entries reject any `.mcp.json` server entry with non-empty `Command`, `URL`, `Type`, `Args`, or `Env`.
       * Cross-platform case-insensitive folding of `COPILOT_HOME` and `ENGRAM_DATA_DIR` environment keys in `.mcp.json` (`strings.EqualFold`) is an **intentional U9 defense-in-depth invariant** (verified by `TestU9_ReviewFixMCPArgumentsAndBindings/casefold-env-*`).
       * Whitespace-padded transport strings (e.g. `" stdio "`), uppercase `"STDIO"` acceptance on `WorkspaceLocalPinned`, interpreter `-m` module flags, OS-specific env-key case sensitivity, YAML `required: null` pointer distinction, and full structured `go.mod`/`go.sum` grammar parsing are **explicitly out of scope for U9** and tracked under P-021 C2 stash entries `00FD5168`, `036B0CB9`, and `48F1E692`.
       * Implementation commit `f078fc3f92cfe67918be5f743a71225a30405ce3` (`internal/toolchain/verify.go`) and test suite `internal/toolchain/verify_test.go` (`a4ec6ead4fea66fe8b6ca95afeabc7c7e49a5cb1`) satisfy the complete U9 contract; Ship proceeds directly to **Step 4.4 Review Gate** (`mode: report-only`) on `212.002-T`, **Step 4.5 Complete Task** (`done`), and **Step 4.6 Wave 2 Convergence Gate**.
     5. **Step 4.4 Review Gate Final Disposition (`READY_WITH_FOLLOWUPS` — Do NOT Re-Invoke `review` on `212.002-T`)**: Task `212.002-T` has completed 4 review-fix cycles (exceeding the 3-cycle cap in `.github/instructions/circuit-breaker.instructions.md`). All remaining findings from commit `18df5aad` fail the P-021 C1 same-contract-surface test (Level 2 binary digest/version-probe verification when `lockfile_path` is omitted, Shipment `214-S` / U12 CLI entrypoint wiring, Python `-m` flag heuristics, OS-specific env-key case sensitivity, YAML `required: null` pointer distinction, transport whitespace/case variants, and `go.mod`/`go.sum` grammar parsing) and are captured under P-021 C2 stash entries `00FD5168`, `036B0CB9`, `48F1E692`, and `9FC3A6A6`, with explicit Stage and operator acknowledgment/deferral rationale (`.github/skills/review/SKILL.md` line 328) and explicit operator residual-risk acceptance. Accordingly, Step 4.4 for `212.002-T` is **SATISFIED (`READY_WITH_FOLLOWUPS`, `P0=0, P1=0` unresolved in-scope findings; follow-ups: `00FD5168, 036B0CB9, 48F1E692, 9FC3A6A6`)**. Ship MUST NOT re-invoke the `review` skill on `212.002-T` and MUST proceed directly to **Step 4.5 (Complete Task -> `done`)** and **Step 4.6 (Wave 2 Convergence Gate)**.

Implement `WriteToolchainManifest(rootPath string, manifest *ToolchainManifest) error`: resolves the expected `<storage_root>/toolchain.yaml` target path and relative tool paths once up front before opening any `*os.Root` pin, validates `manifest` (requiring `SchemaVersion: "1.0"`, `ContainmentLevel: "level-1-workspace-local-pinned"`, `OSContainerSandboxDeferred: true`, non-empty `Tools` and `HostPrerequisites`, and workspace-contained relative paths for all `workspace-local-pinned` tools via the pinned workspace `*os.Root` handle `rootHandle.Lstat(relPath)` + `!core.IsSymlinkOrReparsePoint(info, absPath)`), and writes deterministic YAML to `<storage_root>/toolchain.yaml` via the pinned storage `*os.Root` handle (`storageHandle.Lstat("toolchain.yaml")` + `rejectLink`, `storageHandle.Remove("toolchain.yaml")` when replacing an existing regular file, `storageHandle.OpenFile("toolchain.yaml", os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600)`, and `os.SameFile(opened, linked)` between `f.Stat()` and `storageHandle.Lstat("toolchain.yaml")`).
    2. Implement `RenderPinnedMCPConfig(rootPath string, manifest *ToolchainManifest) ([]byte, error)`: deterministically generates a workspace-pinned `.mcp.json` document from `manifest` where every local MCP server (`backlogit` -> `./bin/backlogit.exe`, `engram` -> `./bin/engram.exe`, `graphtor-docs` -> `./bin/bun.exe` with `.copilot/graphtor-mcp-shim.cjs` and `./bin/kbdb.exe`, `context7` -> `./bin/bun.exe` with `tools/mcp/node_modules/@upstash/context7-mcp/dist/index.js`, `tavily` -> `./bin/bun.exe` with `tools/mcp/node_modules/tavily-mcp/build/index.js`, `github` -> `./bin/github-mcp-server.exe`) is bound to its workspace-local binary/entrypoint with zero bare `PATH` commands, refusing any `..` traversal, external path, or symlink/reparse escape.
  * Create `.backlogit/toolchain.yaml`: the repository's canonical tracked Level 1 workspace toolchain manifest (inventorying `backlogit`, `engram`, `graphtor-docs`, `kbdb`, `context7`, `tavily`, `github`, `bun`, `bunx`, `host_prerequisites` [`pwsh`, `bash`, `git`, `gh`, `go`, `copilot`], `remote-declared` policies, and `built-in-host` tools).
  * Create `tests/integration/multi_session_workspace_containment_test.go`:
    1. `TestU12_WriteToolchainManifestAndRenderPinnedMCPConfigProducesZeroViolations` (`R19`–`R23`, `SC11`–`SC13`): loads the repository's committed `.backlogit/toolchain.yaml` via `LoadToolchainManifest`, verifies 100% tool and `host-prerequisite` / `remote-declared` / `built-in-host` accounting, and provisions a full workspace fixture via `WriteToolchainManifest` + `RenderPinnedMCPConfig` + local `./bin/` and `tools/mcp/` artifacts, proving `AuditRepositoryToolchain` returns `Valid: true, Violations: 0` (`containment_level: "level-1-workspace-local-pinned"`, `os_container_sandbox_deferred: true`).
    2. `TestU12_MultiWorkspaceAllToolsIsolationAndCrossAttachRefusal` (`SC7`, `SC10`, `SC14`): provisions two distinct workspaces (`Workspace A` and `Workspace B`) on the same host under the same user using `WriteToolchainManifest` and `RenderPinnedMCPConfig`, runs simultaneous multi-session `backlogit` daemons and client sessions in both workspaces, verifies distinct IPC endpoints, distinct lock/sidecar files, and zero cross-workspace state bleed, and proves that cross-workspace daemon attach (`ErrDaemonWorkspaceMismatch`) and cross-workspace tool/config paths (`cross_workspace_path_refused`) are deterministically rejected.
    3. `TestU12_NegativePinningGlobalPATHRefusalAndPathTraversalContainment` (`SC8`, `SC9`, `SC12`, `SC14`): proves missing `./bin/backlogit.exe` fails closed even when a fake `backlogit` binary is planted on `PATH`, and proves `..` traversal, absolute external path, and symlink/junction escape attempts across MCP tools, `WriteToolchainManifest`, `RenderPinnedMCPConfig`, and toolchain verification are deterministically refused.

```text
        │
        ├──► (Recorded true-prerequisite blocks edges: BOTH 212-S and 213-S must be shipped before 214-S can be claimed)
        ▼
Shipment 214-S (213-F: Track C Adapters & Negative Suite, U11..U13) [Blocked by 212-S and 213-S]
```

## Risks and Caveats

* **Risk 1: Live Workspace MCP Server Disruption During Testing or Runtime Verification**:
  * *Caveat*: The agent executing Ship for `212-S` / `213-S` / `214-S` communicates with the repository's own live `backlogit` MCP server. Calling `backlogit mcp stop` or `backlogit mcp --replace` against the repository root (`--cwd .`) would terminate the agent's active MCP session.
  * *Mitigation*: Every unit/integration test (`U4`–`U6`, `U11`–`U12`) and every Ship runtime verification probe MUST pass an isolated temporary workspace (`--cwd <tempDir>`).
* **Risk 2: Pre-Existing Dirty Worktree Files (`start.ps1`, `tools/`, `.autoharness/config.yaml`, `.gitignore`, `.backlogit/stash.jsonl` `D895887C`) and Unrelated Lint Captures (`C6E224B8`, `46BD6AEE`, `259A4F71`)**:
  * *Caveat*: `start.ps1`, `tools/`, `.autoharness/config.yaml`, and `.gitignore` are pre-existing uncommitted files in the working tree, and `C6E224B8`, `46BD6AEE`, `259A4F71`, and `D895887C` are unrelated stash entries.
  * *Mitigation*: None of `U1`, `U1b`, or `U2`–`U13` modifies `start.ps1`, `tools/`, `.autoharness/config.yaml`, or `.gitignore`, or folds in `C6E224B8`, `46BD6AEE`, `259A4F71`, or `D895887C`. Under `M1`, when Ship commits the Stage-authored amendment files, Ship stages ONLY the explicit Stage-authored planning and backlog paths (`git add -- <explicit_paths>`) and strictly excludes all pre-existing dirty operator files. `U11` and `U12` inspect launcher and `.mcp.json` files strictly read-only and test modifications inside `t.TempDir()` fixtures. Any future operator adoption of dirty `start.ps1` or `tools/` is governed separately by `210-F` (`workflow-custody/v1` `Profile A`).
* **Risk 3: Known Release Caveat on Predecessor Shipment `211-S` (`210-F`)**:
  * *Caveat*: While `211-S` / `210-F` is shipped and its post-merge closure PR #642 merged to `main` (`5d47e8d4`), `docs/closure/211-S-210-F-post-merge-closure.md` had a shipment-specific operator exception for the closure-evidence gate. That exception does NOT transfer to `212-S`, `213-S`, or `214-S`. Furthermore, staged artifacts committed on a planning branch are NOT on remote `main` until an approved planning PR merges to `main` and Ship's pre-claim gate passes.

## Plan Hardening Signals

* **public API, schema, or contract change**: `present` — Adds `backlogit mcp daemon` and `backlogit mcp prewarm` CLI subcommands, adds `--toolchain` and `--strict` flags to `backlogit mcp status`, extends `MCPDaemonInfo` / `MCPDaemonStatus` JSON schema, adds `schemas/workspace-toolchain.schema.json`, and introduces structured `BusyErrorResponse` MCP error codes.
* **security, auth, permission, or compliance-sensitive behavior**: `present` — Enforces Windows Named Pipe current-user SID DACLs (`TOKEN_USER`), POSIX `0600` socket permissions, pre-attach workspace/binary/version handshake verification (`VerifyMCPDaemonAttachment`), and Level 1 workspace-local tool pinning + `*os.Root` path/reparse checks.
* **migration, backfill, destructive data/config action, or irreversible step**: `present` — `backlogit mcp stop` and `--replace` drain and terminate the verified workspace daemon process holding `mcp-daemon.lock`.
* **external integration, operator checkpoint, or external dependency**: `present` — Defines the cross-tool workspace inventory and Level 1 pinning verification contract across `backlogit`, `engram`, `graphtor-docs`, `context7`, `tavily`, `github-mcp-server`, `bun`, `.mcp.json`, `start.ps1`, and `start.sh`.
* **high runtime, rollout, or rollback risk**: `present` — Modifies the `backlogit mcp` startup and request-dispatch path used by all connected MCP agent sessions.

Requires plan hardening: yes

## Plan Hardening

### 1. Risk Triggers and Protected System-Wide Invariants

* **Invariant I1 (Synchronous-Frame Turn and Resource Ownership — `R9`)**: `OperationCoordinator.Execute` MUST never acquire a mutation turn, read token, or file lock inside a background timeout goroutine (`docs/compound/best-practices/timeout-goroutine-lock-ownership-go-2026-07-01.md`). Every granted mutation turn (`releaseTurn`) and read token MUST be owned in the caller's synchronous stack frame and released via `defer`. If `ctx.Done()` or `admissionTimer.C` fires while queued, the waiter closes `abandoned`, drains any racing grant (`select { case r := <-waiter.grant: close(r) default: }`), and returns `BusyErrorResponse` without executing the handler.
* **Invariant I2 (Deadlock-Free Reader/Writer and Exclusive Barrier Ordering — `R7`, `R8`)**: Normal mutations (`OpMutating`) acquire only the single FIFO mutation turn and allow up to `MaxConcurrentReads` (`4`) concurrent `OpReadOnly` calls against the WAL-mode SQLite pool. Exclusive barrier operations (`OpExclusiveBarrier`: `backlogit_sync_index`) acquire the FIFO mutation turn **first** and then drain all `4` tokens from `readTokens` before calling `db.Rehydrate`. Readers never acquire the mutation turn, so `MutationTurn -> ReadTokens` cannot cycle.
* **Invariant I3 (Non-Exclusive Status and Handshake Probing — `R3`, `D5`, `A045DBA4`)**: `InspectMCPDaemonStatus`, `PrewarmMCPDaemon`, and `RunMCPSessionShim` readiness checks MUST read `"mcp-daemon.json"` via `readMCPDaemonInfoAt(runtimeRoot, runtimeDir)` and verify `isVerifiedProcessAlive` + IPC `"status"` ping **without** calling `tryLockFile` on `mcp-daemon.lock`. `openMCPDaemonLockFile(runtimeRoot, runtimeDir)` is called by `InspectMCPDaemonStatus` only when `"mcp-daemon.json"` exists on disk and `isVerifiedProcessAlive` confirms the recorded process is dead.
* **Invariant I4 (Per-Session Isolation and First-Client Disconnect Survival — `R4`–`R6`, `R13`)**: Each IPC connection registers a unique `MultiSessionConn` (`sessionID`) on `s.mcp`. Request IDs (`id`), `notifications/cancelled` cancellation contexts, and `NotificationChannel()` streams are scoped strictly per `sessionID`. Disconnecting any client session (including the first client that triggered lazy daemon auto-start) unregisters only that `sessionID` and never terminates the daemon while other sessions remain active or within `idleTimeout`.
* **Invariant I5 (Pre-Attach Identity Verification and Zero Silent Global `PATH` Fallback — `R15`, `SC7`, `SC8`, `SC12`)**: Before attaching a session or completing prewarm, the caller MUST verify canonical `WorkspaceRoot`, `StorageRoot`, `ExecutablePath`, and `Version`. Any workspace mismatch (`ErrDaemonWorkspaceMismatch`), version/binary mismatch without `--replace` (`ErrDaemonVersionMismatch`), or missing workspace-pinned binary (`ErrDaemonBinaryMissing`) MUST fail closed without adopting a foreign daemon, without killing active peer sessions, and without falling back to global `PATH`.
* **Invariant I6 (Handle-Pinned Runtime Directory, Verified Absolute Reparse Validation Path, Workspace-Contained POSIX UDS, and Same-User IPC Access Control — `R3`, `R16`, `085-DL`, Constitution Principle IV)**: All `<storage_root>/runtime` file and socket operations (`mcp-daemon.lock`, `mcp-daemon.json`, and POSIX `mcp.sock` / short fallback `s`) execute relative to the pinned `*os.Root` handle (`openMCPDaemonRuntimeRoot`, with expected paths resolved once up front before opening the pin per `docs/compound/2026-10-27-pin-the-handle-not-the-path.md`), while every `rejectLink` (`core.IsSymlinkOrReparsePoint`) call across `AcquireMCPDaemonLock`, `InspectMCPDaemonStatus`, `StopMCPDaemon`, and holder metadata read/reverification seams receives the upfront-verified absolute `runtimeDir` leaf path (documented platform distinction: on POSIX `isSymlinkOrReparse` inspects only the handle-relative `info.Mode()&os.ModeSymlink != 0` and ignores the path string; on Windows `windows.GetFileAttributes(absLeafPath)` is the required reparse-attribute supplement because Go 1.24's `os.FileInfo.Mode()` reports NTFS junctions as `ModeIrregular`, and must never receive a relative `runtimeRoot.Name()` string from `storageHandle.OpenRoot("runtime")` — `docs/compound/runtime-errors/nested-os-root-name-is-not-an-absolute-path-2026-11-06.md`) and retains `os.SameFile(opened, linked)` opened-handle to handle-relative-`Lstat` object identity verification (`U1b` / `211.008-T`). Windows Named Pipes (`\\.\pipe\backlogit-mcp-<hexHash16>`) enforce a current-user SID `DACL` and `PIPE_REJECT_REMOTE_CLIENTS`; POSIX sockets enforce `0600` mode strictly inside `<storage_root>/runtime/` (`mcp.sock`, or short leaf `s` when `len(mcp.sock) > 100 && len(s) <= 100`), verify `filepath.Clean(filepath.Dir(endpoint)) == filepath.Clean(runtimeDir)` upfront equality and handle-relative `runtimeRoot.Lstat(leafName)` socket identity, and fail closed with wrapped `corerrors.ErrValidation` when `len(s) > 100` (never writing to `/tmp`, `$TMPDIR`, `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR`, or `$HOME`).
* **Invariant I7 (Level 1 All-Tools Provisioning, Config Rendering, Honest Reporting, and Threat-Model Boundary — `R18`–`R23`, `SC10`–`SC14`)**: `WriteToolchainManifest` and `RenderPinnedMCPConfig` deterministically produce `.backlogit/toolchain.yaml` and workspace-pinned `.mcp.json` configurations where every local tool resolves under `./bin/`, `.copilot/`, or `tools/mcp/node_modules/` with zero bare `PATH` commands; `VerifyWorkspaceToolchain` and `AuditRepositoryToolchain` account for 100% of tools in `.mcp.json`, `start.ps1`, and `start.sh` (`SC11`), classify unavoidable bootstrap tools as `host-prerequisite`, classify approved network-backed MCP tools as `remote-declared`, flag bare-command or missing local binaries as `non-conformant` or `unsupported` (`SC13`), and record `containment_level: "level-1-workspace-local-pinned"` with `os_container_sandbox_deferred: true` (`R18`, `R23`).
* **Invariant I8 (Live Workspace MCP Server and Pre-Existing Dirty File Preservation)**: No test or verification command may run `StopMCPDaemon` or `--replace` against the live repository root (`C:\Source\GitHub\backlogit`), and no task in `212-S`, `213-S`, or `214-S` may overwrite, stage, or discard pre-existing dirty operator files (`start.ps1`, `tools/`, `.autoharness/config.yaml`, `.gitignore`, or stash entries `C6E224B8`, `46BD6AEE`, `259A4F71`, `D895887C`).

### 2. Reinforced Instructions and Institutional Learnings Consulted

* `docs/compound/2026-07-07-empty-head-fail-closed-repo-presence-probe.md` and `docs/compound/best-practices/git-aware-backlog-artifact-archival-preserves-follow-history-2026-07-10.md` — Check `runCtx.Err() != nil` before exit codes on Windows, keep `withCLocale(gate.MinimalEnv())` and `isGitOverrideEnv()` sanitization intact, and enforce fail-closed validation when a `.git` entry in `[workspaceRoot, workTreeRoot)` is bypassed in favor of an ancestor repository (`U1c`, `U1d`).
* `docs/compound/2026-07-06-external-process-timeout-before-probe.md`, `docs/compound/2026-07-06-autoharness-gate-broker-integration-contract.md`, `docs/compound/2026-07-20-ship-gate-descoped-archived-member-exemption.md`, `docs/compound/2026-10-11-unfiltered-suite-evidence-under-load.md`, `docs/compound/best-practices/windows-safe-atomic-rename-goos-gate-2026-04-23.md`, and `docs/compound/2026-08-29-windows-preremove-rename-pattern.md` — Keep `ExecVersionRunner`, `ExecRunner`, and `ExecGitRunner` context-bounded while isolating `ExecGitRunner.Verify` from ancestor checkouts under workspace-contained `os.TempDir()` via `GIT_CEILING_DIRECTORIES` and caching successful `ExecVersionRunner.Version` probes (`U1k` / `211.022-T`), disable the unconfigured external `autoharness` subprocess `GateBroker` and complete activated shipment task members via `core.UpdateArtifact` before `ShipShipment` in non-gate Doctor and integration release fixtures (`U1e`), and verify `96F1DFFD` (`MoveFileEx` in `atomicfile_windows.go` and `NtSetInformationFile` in `shipment_ops_windows.go`) via an uncached unfiltered full-suite run under quiet-host conditions with `-json`/`-v` named-failure capture (noting archived `885263D2` and `D8EF5443`).

* `docs/compound/best-practices/timeout-goroutine-lock-ownership-go-2026-07-01.md` — Own admission turns, read tokens, and locks in the synchronous caller frame via `defer`, never inside an abandoned timeout goroutine (`U1`, `U2`).
* `docs/compound/2026-07-04-core-extraction-shared-eventwriter-append-serialization.md` — Keep `s.Events` as the single shared `*events.EventWriter` instance across all sessions on `Server` (`U2`, `U3`).
* `docs/compound/2026-10-06-ship-shipment-mcp-timeout-result-unknown.md` — Prevent head-of-line read starvation behind long-running mutations (`backlogit_ship_shipment`) by allowing bounded concurrent reads (`OpReadOnly`) alongside FIFO-gated writes (`OpMutating`) (`U2`).
* `docs/compound/2026-10-27-pin-the-handle-not-the-path.md` — Resolve expected paths once up front before opening any `*os.Root` pin (never calling `EvalSymlinks` inside an open pin window), retain `*os.Root` handle-relative `Lstat`/`Open`/`Remove` operations on `<storage_root>/runtime` and toolchain paths, and verify `os.SameFile(opened, linked)` against handle-relative `Lstat` (`U1b`, `U4`, `U5`, `U9`, `U12`).
* `docs/compound/runtime-errors/nested-os-root-name-is-not-an-absolute-path-2026-11-06.md` — Never assume a nested `os.Root.Name()` (`storageHandle.OpenRoot("runtime")`) is canonical or absolute; propagate the upfront-verified absolute `runtimeDir` to path-based OS reparse checks (`rejectLink` -> `windows.GetFileAttributes`) while keeping file operations relative to `runtimeRoot` and `os.SameFile(opened, linked)` intact (`U1b`, `U4`, `U5`), and capture subprocess `stderr` in `U6` subprocess helpers (`mcp_session_shim_test.go`) when diagnosing readiness EOF.
* `docs/compound/build-errors/match-ci-go-and-linter-before-source-repair-2026-11-06.md` — Inspect `go.mod`, CI pins, and lint configuration before source edits; select the cached workspace Go 1.24.0 toolchain (`GOROOT` + leading `PATH`, `GOTOOLCHAIN=local`, `GOFLAGS=-mod=readonly`, `GOPROXY=off`, `GOSUMDB=off`, and workspace-contained build/module/lint/GOPATH/temp caches on each fresh shell invocation) and pinned `bin/golangci-lint.exe` (v1.64.8), recording compiler, linter, native exit, and complete diagnostic inventory while keeping unsupported-version (e.g., v2) findings separate from supported gate outcomes across all Go units (`U1`, `U1b`, `U2`–`U13`).
* `docs/compound/2026-10-19-lock-held-side-effect-ordering.md` — Place `OperationCoordinator` outside `.backlogit/.locks/` (`MutationTurn -> ReadTokens` and `OperationCoordinator -> .backlogit/.locks/ -> SQLite BEGIN IMMEDIATE`) so lock acquisition orders never invert (`U2`), and audit all reachable locks inside `planReconstitution` (`readShipmentFromDiskReadOnly`, `findArtifact`, `scanCanonicalArtifacts`, `events.ReadAllEvents` — zero mutexes or file locks acquired) before moving `executeReconstitution`'s preliminary `planReconstitution` read inside `stash -> lifecycle-global -> shipment-membership` (`U1f` / `211.012-T`).
* `docs/compound/concurrency-issues/2026-08-09-stable-lock-keys-and-heartbeat-refresh-for-unbounded-holds.md`, `docs/compound/2026-07-28-durable-writes-two-class-contract-commit-then-surface.md`, `docs/compound/2026-07-29-fsutil-neutral-leaf-caller-classifies.md`, and `docs/compound/2026-07-29-durable-writes-test-seam-patterns.md` — Preserve stable `.backlogit/.locks/<id>` synthetic lock keys (rejecting the reserved `shipment-lifecycle-global` lock key and case-folded duplicate `source_shipments` IDs in `loadAndValidateReconstitutePlan` before `lockShipmentMembership`), `20s` heartbeat / `60s` stale-TTL / `3s` `defaultGateLockBoundedWait` semantics, two-class write error classification (`ErrWriteNotApplied` vs `ErrWriteIndeterminate` with no blind retry and no rollback on indeterminate), and non-parallel `sync.Once`-guarded `artifactMutationLockBarrierHook` / `tryLockActiveStash` test seams in `U1f` (`211.012-T`).
* `docs/compound/2026-07-06-exec-binary-config-must-be-bare-path-validated.md` and `docs/compound/2026-07-06-bounded-helper-timeout-hard-cap.md` — Use argv-array-only `exec.CommandContext` (never shell interpolation) and hard-capped handshake/status timeouts (`500ms`–`10s`) when spawning or probing `backlogit mcp daemon` (`U5`, `U6`, `U9`).
* `docs/compound/2026-08-31-duplicate-yaml-keys-survive-safe-load-validation.md` — Reject duplicate YAML/JSON keys when loading `.backlogit/toolchain.yaml` and `.mcp.json` (`U9`, `U11`).
* `docs/compound/best-practices/source-shape-harnesses-must-allow-lifecycle-successors-2026-09-11.md` — `U1`, `U3a`, `U4a`, `U5a`, and `U8` `go/ast` source-shape harnesses assert permanent type, tag, and signature contracts rather than temporary `ErrNotImplemented` bodies.
* `docs/compound/2026-06-26-docline-frontmatter-contract.md` and `docs/compound/workflow-issues/cli-reference-drift-check-manual-edits-bypass-gen-docs-2026-04-25.md` — Regenerate `docs/cli-reference/` via `go run ./cmd/gen-docs docs/cli-reference` in the same shipment that modifies CLI commands/flags (`U7` in `212-S` and `U13` in `214-S`) and self-lint all docs via `go run ./cmd/backlogit docs lint`.
* `docs/compound/2026-07-20-manual-feature-harvest-provenance-backfill.md` — Harvest `ECF33C79` once via `backlogit_harvest_stash` into `211-F` so canonical `source_stash_*`, `source_deliberation_id: 082-DL`, and `.backlogit/archive/stash.jsonl` (`reason: "harvested"`, `harvested_artifact_id: "211-F"`) are populated without duplicate harvests.

* **Operator-Owned Verified Workspace Toolchain Setup, Provisioning, and Custody Alignment Procedure (`R19`–`R23`, `D9`)**:
  1. **Ownership & Gitignore/Custody Boundary**:
     * **Ship agent (`213-S` + `214-S`)**: Commits `schemas/workspace-toolchain.schema.json`, `internal/toolchain/{types,verify,launcher}.go`, `.backlogit/toolchain.yaml`, `docs/design-docs/2026-11-04-multi-session-mcp-runtime-and-workspace-toolchain.md`, `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`, `docs/configuration.md`, and `docs/cli-reference/backlogit_mcp*.md`.
     * **Operator (`210-F` `workflow-custody/v1` Profile A + gitignored local runtime provisioning)**: Owns pre-existing uncommitted worktree files (`start.ps1`, `.gitignore`, `tools/mcp/package.json`, `tools/mcp/bun.lock`, `tools/mcp/install.ps1`) and gitignored local files (`./bin/*.exe`, `.copilot/graphtor-mcp-shim.cjs`, `tools/mcp/node_modules/`, `.mcp.json`).
  2. **Per-Tool Source, Version, Checksum/Lockfile Provenance, and Workspace Binding**:
     * `backlogit` (`workspace-local-pinned`): built from repo source (`go build -o ./bin/backlogit.exe ./cmd/backlogit`), version matched to `version.Resolve()`, SHA-256 recorded in `.backlogit/toolchain.yaml`, invoked as `./bin/backlogit.exe mcp` in `.mcp.json` and `$workspaceBinDirectory\backlogit.exe` (`sync` + `mcp prewarm`) in `start.ps1`/`start.sh`.
     * `engram` (`workspace-local-pinned`): provisioned at `./bin/engram.exe`, version + SHA-256 pinned in `.backlogit/toolchain.yaml`, bound to `ENGRAM_DATA_DIR=<workspace>/.engram`, invoked as `./bin/engram.exe shim` in `.mcp.json`.
     * `bun` and `bunx` (`workspace-local-pinned`): provisioned at `./bin/bun.exe` and `./bin/bunx.exe`, version + SHA-256 pinned in `.backlogit/toolchain.yaml`, isolated via `BUN_INSTALL=<workspace>/bin/bun`, `BUN_INSTALL_CACHE_DIR=<workspace>/bin/bun-cache`, `BUN_RUNTIME_TRANSPILER_CACHE_PATH=0`.
     * `context7` and `tavily` (`workspace-local-pinned` + `remote_policy: "remote-declared"`): pinned in `tools/mcp/package.json` (`@upstash/context7-mcp: "4.4.4"`, `tavily-mcp: "0.2.22"`, `@opentelemetry/api: "1.9.1"`) and `tools/mcp/bun.lock`, installed via `pwsh -NoProfile -File tools/mcp/install.ps1` (`./bin/bun.exe install --cwd tools/mcp --frozen-lockfile --ignore-scripts --backend copyfile --no-progress`, with `tools/mcp/install.ps1` line 3 updated from `.bin` to `bin`), and invoked in `.mcp.json` via `"command": "./bin/bun.exe"` with `"args": ["tools/mcp/node_modules/@upstash/context7-mcp/dist/index.js"]` and `["tools/mcp/node_modules/tavily-mcp/build/index.js"]`.
     * `github` (`workspace-local-pinned` + `remote_policy: "remote-declared"`): provisioned at `./bin/github-mcp-server.exe`, version + SHA-256 pinned in `.backlogit/toolchain.yaml`, invoked via `"command": "./bin/github-mcp-server.exe"`, `"args": ["stdio"]` in `.mcp.json`.
     * `graphtor-docs` / `kbdb` (`workspace-local-pinned`): provisioned at `.copilot/graphtor-mcp-shim.cjs` + `./bin/kbdb.exe`, SHA-256 pinned in `.backlogit/toolchain.yaml`, invoked in `.mcp.json` via `"command": "./bin/bun.exe"`, `"args": [".copilot/graphtor-mcp-shim.cjs", "./bin/kbdb.exe", "serve", "--read-only"]` (read-only workspace policy enforced).
     * `host-prerequisite` (`pwsh`, `bash`, `git`, `gh`, `go`, `copilot`) and `built-in-host` (IDE/CLI native tools): explicitly declared in `.backlogit/toolchain.yaml` and reported transparently (`os_container_sandbox_deferred: true`).
  3. **Runtime Acceptance Check**:
     * After running `tools/mcp/install.ps1` and writing `RenderPinnedMCPConfig` output to `.mcp.json`, `./bin/backlogit.exe mcp status --toolchain --strict --format json` MUST exit `0` with `valid: true`, `violations: 0`, `containment_level: "level-1-workspace-local-pinned"`, and `os_container_sandbox_deferred: true`.
* **Environment Prechecks and Runtime Verification Protocol (Per Shipment)**:
  1. **Mandatory isolated workspace rule**: Never invoke `backlogit mcp stop` or `backlogit mcp --replace` against `C:\Source\GitHub\backlogit` during Ship execution; always pass `--cwd <isolatedTempDir>`.
  2. **Shipment `212-S` (`211-F`) verification**:
     * Build `./bin/backlogit.exe` (in an isolated temp output path during verification) and run `backlogit --cwd <tempDir> mcp prewarm --format json`, two concurrent `backlogit --cwd <tempDir> mcp` stdio client sessions issuing overlapping `"id": 1` read/write tool calls, `backlogit --cwd <tempDir> mcp status --format json` (confirming `active_sessions: 2`), disconnect Client 1 (confirming `active_sessions: 1` and Client 2 health), and `backlogit --cwd <tempDir> mcp stop --format json` (confirming `stopped: true, running: false`).
     * Run `go test -timeout=30m -count=1 -race ./internal/mcp/... ./internal/cli/... ./internal/errors/...` and the full repository suite `go test -timeout=30m ./...`.
  3. **Shipment `213-S` (`212-F`) verification**:
     * Run `go test -timeout=30m -count=1 -race ./internal/toolchain/...`, `go run ./cmd/backlogit docs lint`, and `go test -timeout=30m ./...`.
  4. **Shipment `214-S` (`213-F`) verification**:
     * Run `backlogit --cwd <tempDir> mcp status --toolchain --format json` against both a fully pinned temp workspace (`Violations == 0`) and an unpinned/shadow-tool temp workspace (`Violations > 0`), plus `go test -timeout=30m -count=1 -race ./internal/toolchain/... ./internal/cli/... ./tests/integration/...` and `go test -timeout=30m ./...`.
* **Operational Closure Expectations**:
  * **Owner**: Ship agent (`_Ship`) for each shipment (`212-S`, `213-S`, `214-S`), supervised by Orchestrator (`_Orchestrator`).
  * **Validation window**: Immediate post-merge verification on `main` plus 7-day observation window across multi-agent sessions (`_Orchestrator`, `_Stage`, `_Ship`).
  * **Monitoring signals**:
    * `backlogit mcp status --format json` (`running`, `pid`, `active_sessions`, `ipc_transport`, `endpoint`);
    * Structured `BusyErrorResponse` telemetry (`mutation_queue_full`, `mutation_admission_timeout`) and zero `-32001` read timeouts during concurrent `backlogit_ship_shipment` / `backlogit_sync_index` execution;
    * `backlogit mcp status --toolchain --format json` (`violations`, per-tool `containment_class` and `status`);
    * `backlogit doctor` reporting `0` errors.
  * **Rollback triggers and procedure**:
    * *Trigger*: Spurious `ErrDaemonAlreadyRunning` on multi-agent startup, cross-session response/notification misrouting, deadlocked `OperationCoordinator` queue, or `CLI Reference Drift Check` / `Docline frontmatter gate` failure on `main`.
    * *Procedure*: Stop any affected temp/workspace daemon (`backlogit mcp stop`), revert the offending shipment merge commit on a reviewed PR via `git revert -m 1 <merge_sha>`, rebuild `bin/backlogit.exe`, and run `backlogit sync`. Because `mcp-daemon.json` and IPC endpoints are ephemeral under `<storage_root>/runtime/` and `.backlogit/toolchain.yaml` is additive, rollback requires zero database or Markdown migration.
  * **Closure artifacts**:
    * `docs/closure/212-S-211-F-post-merge-closure.md`
    * `docs/closure/213-S-212-F-post-merge-closure.md`
    * `docs/closure/214-S-213-F-post-merge-closure.md`
    * Each closure must invoke `compact-context` (`target: all`) per P-020 and satisfy the closure-evidence gate on its own merits (the shipment-specific operator exception on `211-S` does NOT transfer to `212-S`, `213-S`, or `214-S`).

* round_1_remediation_log:
  * **P2-1 (`Architecture Strategist` + `Agent-Native Parity Reviewer`, `U11` `213.001-T`)**: Replaced editing `MCPDaemonStatus` in `internal/cli/mcp_daemon.go` with an output wrapper struct `mcpStatusToolchainOutput` in `internal/cli/root.go` (preserving `U11`'s 3-file cap and decoupling `mcp_daemon.go` from `internal/toolchain`), and specified writing the JSON/text report before returning the wrapped validation error under `--strict`.
  * **P2-2 (`Architecture Strategist`, `U2` `211.002-T`)**: Enforced the lazy-workspace-initialization barrier (`R8`) inside `internal/mcp/server.go` at the `addTool` / `InvokeTool` wrapper boundary and `s.backlogitDir()` under `s.workspaceMu`, avoiding edits to `dynamic.go`, `hook_tools.go`, or `metadata.go` while keeping `U2` within its 3-file cap.
  * **P2-3 (`Go Reviewer` + `Agent-Native Parity Reviewer`, `U1`/`U2` `211.001-T`/`211.002-T`)**: Pinned `BusyErrorResponse.Error` to `"server_busy"`, added `"read_admission_timeout"` to the closed `BusyErrorResponse.Code` set, specified a `closed chan struct{}` shutdown signal in `(*OperationCoordinator).Close()`, and required `OpExclusiveBarrier` to release any partially acquired `readTokens` via its synchronous-frame `defer` on timeout or cancellation.
  * **P2-4 (`Go Reviewer`, `U3` `211.003-T`)**: Specified `bufio.Reader` (`ReadBytes('\n')` with `4 MiB` max frame guard), a per-session `sync.WaitGroup` joining all request and notification goroutines before `ServeSessionStream` returns, and typed `canonicalRequestIDKey` normalization instead of `fmt.Sprintf("%v", id)`.
  * **P2-5 (`Go Reviewer`, `U4` `211.004-T`)**: Specified `CreateNamedPipe` handle/overlapped semantics and `windows.WaitNamedPipe` retry on `ERROR_PIPE_BUSY` in `DialMCPDaemonIPC`.
  * **P2-6 (`Go Reviewer` + `Architecture Strategist`, `U5` `211.005-T`)**: Retained lock-free `runtimeRoot.Lstat(mcpDaemonLockName)` + `rejectLink` in `InspectMCPDaemonStatus` (preserving `TestU2_RejectsSymlinkOrReparseRuntimeDir/lock_leaf_symlink`), defaulted `serveMCPStdio` to `nil` in `internal/cli/root.go` for `TestU3_*` compatibility, passed the handshake `*bufio.Reader` into `ServeSessionStream` so pipelined `initialize` bytes are preserved, and added non-exclusive initial check + bounded (`500ms`) IPC `"stop"` graceful drain to `StopMCPDaemon`.
  * **P3-1 through P3-8 (`Constitution Reviewer`, `Go Reviewer`, `Architecture Strategist`, `Agent-Native Parity Reviewer`)**: Added exact function signatures in `U1`/`U8`, exact struct JSON/YAML tag lists and `package toolchain` RED-phase declaration in `U8`, `"backlogit mcp"` prefix parity assertion in `U6`, `git diff --exit-code docs/cli-reference/` in `U7`, `## Dependency Direction` rules in `U10`, and P-008 (`make md-lint`) + 3-file TDD bound + POSIX `sun_path` fallback notes in `## Constitution Check`.

  * **Check 1 (`R19`/`R23` Local Installation, Configuration, and Version Pinning vs Audit-Only Proxy — `D9`, `U10`, `U12` `213.002-T`, `U13` `213.003-T`, `Invariant I7`)**: Upgraded `U12` (`213.002-T`) from a `tests`-only audit unit to a `code` unit implementing `WriteToolchainManifest(rootPath string, manifest *ToolchainManifest) error` and `RenderPinnedMCPConfig(rootPath string, manifest *ToolchainManifest) ([]byte, error)` in `internal/toolchain/launcher.go` and committing the repository's canonical tracked `.backlogit/toolchain.yaml` manifest, paired with the actionable 4-step **Operator-Owned Verified Workspace Toolchain Setup, Provisioning, and Custody Alignment Procedure** in `D9`, `U10`, `U13`, and `## Runtime Verification and Closure` (ownership, per-tool version/SHA-256/`tools/mcp/bun.lock` provenance, `tools/mcp/install.ps1` `bin` directory fix + `--frozen-lockfile` provisioning, gitignored `.mcp.json` alignment via `RenderPinnedMCPConfig`, and `backlogit mcp status --toolchain --strict` runtime acceptance).
  * **Check 2 (`R16` / Constitution Principle IV POSIX IPC Workspace Containment — `D2`, `U4` `211.004-T`, `## Constitution Check`, `Invariant I6`)**: Removed the outside-workspace `<tempOrXdgRuntimeDir>/backlogit-<uid>/mcp-<hexHash16>.sock` fallback. `DeriveMCPDaemonEndpoint` and `ListenMCPDaemonIPC` on POSIX now keep all Unix Domain Sockets strictly inside `<storage_root>/runtime/` pinned by `runtimeRoot` (`*os.Root`): primary `<storage_root>/runtime/mcp.sock` (`<=100` bytes), workspace-contained short leaf `<storage_root>/runtime/s` when `len(mcp.sock) > 100 && len(s) <= 100`, and deterministic `corerrors.ErrValidation` refusal with actionable remediation guidance and zero `/tmp`, `$TMPDIR`, `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR`, or `$HOME` writes when `len(s) > 100`.
  * **Check 3 (`U5`/`U6`/`U12` Test-First / Atomic Parity and Registry Parity Ordering — `D6`, `U5` `211.005-T`, `U6` `211.006-T`, `U12` `213.002-T`)**: Restructured `U5` (`211.005-T`) and `U6` (`211.006-T`) so both are `code` units with genuine compiling-but-failing RED phases and zero breakage of `TestRegistryParity_*`: `U5` (`3` files after `089-DL` / `0D073C21`: `internal/mcp/session_mux.go`, `internal/cli/mcp_daemon.go`, `internal/cli/mcp_daemon_multisession_test.go`) closes `s.coordinator` inside `(*Server).CloseAllSessions()` after draining sessions and implements `RunMCPDaemon`, `VerifyMCPDaemonAttachment`, non-exclusive `InspectMCPDaemonStatus`, and graceful-drain `StopMCPDaemon` without touching `internal/cli/root.go`; `U6` (`3` files: `internal/cli/mcp_daemon.go`, `internal/cli/root.go`, `internal/cli/mcp_session_shim_test.go`) implements `PrewarmMCPDaemon` and `RunMCPSessionShim` in `mcp_daemon.go` and wires `backlogit mcp`, `backlogit mcp daemon`, and `backlogit mcp prewarm` in `root.go` with unit + cross-process subprocess + CLI-only parity tests in `mcp_session_shim_test.go`.
